- **The whole application is the unit.** §3's four properties (composition,
  locality, continuity, performance) are the right acceptance vocabulary.
  Keep them as the headings of the scenario suite.
- **Ownership before abstraction.** §5's table is the best paragraph in the

before readiness. A parked-constructor witness fails before the change; the
final 30-loan burst never enters construction and takes at most 19.95 µs to
acquire a loan. Constructor unwind and epoch replacement preserve reservations.

The local cold baseline reproduces the first-cohort counter signature: five
failed arms each lose 32 HTTP acknowledgements while exactly those 32 writes
commit (160 total). Across 78 prepared-reader trials, 1,079,999 acknowledged
writes exactly reconcile with durable commits; one overloaded request is
E_RATE_LIMIT, with no wire timeouts or extra commits. A controlled post-COMMIT
HTTP timeout, including keyed replay, confirms correct unknown-outcome semantics.
The original native 134 excess commits have the same signature; their individual
request attribution remains unavailable from the retained aggregate alone.

Collection keeps FIFO, N≤32 and the original 1 ms window. A 20 ms arrival-history
horizon spans a busy write cycle; non-idle waits use one predicted interarrival
interval, bounded by that window. Idle arrivals do not wait. The first 1 ms
history candidate incorrectly treated queued bursts as idle; a failing-before
policy witness catches it. All three bounded policy arms are retained. Final
saturated fill stays near N=16; one-member groups fall sharply at 9,600/s, but
throughput moves in opposite directions at the two saturated rungs. No reliable
throughput improvement or S1 qualification is claimed on this noisy host.

Fenced BEGIN and first facts share one message, with a raising catalog-qualified
owner check before head/data commands and the original remaining budget before
the owner lock. A held-owner witness catches a misplaced timeout; a shadow
operator catches an unqualified comparison. Grant-enabled tails return their
publication stamp from the head UPDATE's RETURNING relation. The gate still
closes before separate uncancellable COMMIT and installs only on confirmation.
Simple batches fall G5 5→4 / G6 6→4 flights; the shared-dependency core case
falls 6→5. Both stores' HTTP/core RT0 ratchets pass; SQLite's values are unchanged.

# Rules

**Snapback 2 optimizes the edit-to-verified loop — the agent's building an app on it, and ours building it.**

Speed is the constraint. Everything below exists to keep the edit-to-verified loop short
and to stop this repository accreting apparatus faster than anyone can remove it. These
rules bind `snapback2/` and the LLPs numbered 1000 and up; Snapback 1 is baselined, not
exempt, and its numbers may only fall.

Each rule is **[check]** (enforced by `caps`) or **[review]** (enforced by a human).

## Budgets — every budget is a trade, not a limit

- **5 blocking checks, 60s total.** A sixth requires deleting one, in the same PR. **[check]**
- **59 documents in the working set** (`llp/current/`). Today's count. A 60th requires
  archiving one — which is just removing the link. Snapback 2 aims to leave this at 15;
  Snapback 1's links are archived at promotion. **[check]**
- **12 documents in the foundation** (`llp/foundation/`). **[check]**
- **This file: 700 words.** If it grows, something becomes a check or stops being a rule. **[check]**
- **1,500 lines per source file, baseline 204.** Generated files exempt — and generated
  files are built, not committed; so are benchmark run records under `benchmarks/runs/`,
  which are evidence, not source. The baseline is Snapback 1's oversize count on the day
  the rule landed; it may not grow, and `snapback2/` contributes zero to it. **[check]**

## Blocking checks

`caps` · `ref-check` · `snapback2/scripts/test.sh` · `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs --check` · `check-inventories`

## Loop shape

- **Every check reports all failures in one run.** Fail-fast is banned. **[check]**
- **Nothing blocks on anything slower than 60 seconds.** Slow verification runs on a
  schedule, attributed to the breaking commit, and files a bounded set of issues. **[review]**
- **Fix loops get 3 rounds.** Then stop: escalate to a human or descope. **[review]**

## Time budgets

Tracked every run of `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs` (appends `snapback2/metrics/loop.jsonl`).
A regression is a P0 with a name on it.

| | budget |
|---|---|
| Incremental rebuild after a one-line edit | 15s |
| Test what you just changed | 30s |
| Blocking gate suite | 60s |
| Full build, warm cache | 5 min |
| **`snapback2 check` on a program (the agent's product latency)** | **1s** |
| **`snapback2 doctor` boot-to-ready** | **0.5s** |

## Scope

- **`NOT-DOING.md` is binding.** Moving something onto the doing-list means writing why
  and taking something off. **[review]**
- **Delete; don't deprecate.** No compat shims or migration paths before promotion. **[review]**
- **Nothing is compiled, transpiled, or generated at runtime that could be built ahead of
  time.** **[review]**
- **A spec needs an implementer and a date, or it isn't written.** **[review]**
- **Only implemented specs bind.** A document with no running code behind it is a
  proposal, and a proposal cannot block a PR or justify a check. **[review]**

## Agents

- **Agents remove apparatus freely and add none.** An agent PR that adds a check, script,
  registry, config file, or design doc needs explicit human approval saying so. **[review]**
- **2026-09-06 session exception:** the user delegated the search implementation
  decisions; one `snapback2-core/build.rs` may generate pinned Unicode tables at
  build time, alongside the existing package build. No new gate, registry,
  runtime generator, or Unicode dependency is authorized by this exception. **[review]**
- **Snapback 2 LLPs are numbered 1000 and up**; `llp-create` must be told. **[review]**
- **Snapback 4 LLPs are numbered 3000 and up** (`snapback4/`, LLP 3000); it adopts these budgets
  and loop shape as its own and keeps its scope in `snapback4/NOT-DOING.md`. **[review]**

## Shared machines

- Warm, named lanes, one per stream of work. Never clone fresh; reuse the worktree.
- **Never `git stash`.** Use a temporary commit.
- Kill only PIDs you recorded at launch. Never by name or pattern.

//! One-child historical extraction moves the existing asset home atomically.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — deterministic identities, no upload or second home.
use super::*;
#[path = "extraction_each.rs"]
mod each;
use crate::{
    meter::{Ceilings, Cut, Meter},
    schema::{ColumnType, Extraction},
};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
fn refuse(s: impl Into<String>) -> RuntimeError {
    RuntimeError::Structural {
        code: "E_ADOPT",
        message: s.into(),
    }
}
fn asset(k: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    match k {
        ColumnType::Optional(k)
        | ColumnType::List { of: k, .. }
        | ColumnType::ListRange { of: k, .. } => asset(k),
        ColumnType::Image { .. } | ColumnType::File { .. } | ColumnType::Video { .. } => true,
        _ => false,
    }
}
// Closed audience inclusion: equality, deny, and Boolean conjunction/union.
// This never infers a business relation between different atomic predicates.
fn audience_subset(next:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,source:&crate::schema::RuleExpr)->bool{
    use crate::schema::RuleExpr::*;
    next==source || *next==Deny || *source==Allow || match (next,source) {
        (Or(xs),_)=>xs.iter().all(|x|audience_subset(x,source)),
        (_,And(xs))=>xs.iter().all(|x|audience_subset(next,x)),
        (And(xs),_)=>xs.iter().any(|x|audience_subset(x,source)),
        (_,Or(xs))=>xs.iter().any(|x|audience_subset(next,x)),
        _=>false,
    }
}
fn mapped_audience(parent:&Table,child:&Table,e:&Extraction,rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr)->crate::schema::RuleExpr{
    let mut transported=parent.clone();
    let names=parent.columns.keys().map(|source|{
        let copy=e.fields.iter().find(|(_,old)|*old==source).filter(|(dest,_)|!asset(&child.columns[*dest])&&child.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,crate::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields}if fields.contains(*dest))));
        (source.clone(),copy.map(|(dest,_)|dest.clone()).unwrap_or_else(||format!("$extraction-source:{source}")))
    }).collect();
    transported.column_reads=BTreeMap::from([("$gate".into(),rule.clone())]);
    super::asset_renames::table(&mut transported,&names);
    transported.column_reads.remove("$gate").unwrap()
}
fn probe_context(rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,next:&Schema)->bool{
    use crate::schema::RuleExpr::*;
    match rule {
        Exists{table,index,..}|Created{table,index,..}=>sources.get(table).zip(next.table(table)).is_some_and(|(old,new)|old.index(index)==new.index(index)&&old.rules.read==new.rules.read),
        And(xs)|Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(|r|probe_context(r,sources,next)),Not(r)=>probe_context(r,sources,next),_=>true,
    }
}
fn fields(row: &mut Value) -> Result<&mut crate::value::ObjectFields, RuntimeError> {
    match row {
        Value::Row(f) | Value::Map(f) => Ok(f),
        _ => Err(refuse("invalid extraction row")),
    }
}
fn key(tx:&Tx<'_>,e:&Extraction)->Result<String,RuntimeError>{
    let parent=tx.num(&e.parent)?;let child=tx.num(&e.child)?;
    let aliases:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_asset_column_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    let origin=|t:i64,c:&str|->Result<String,RuntimeError>{
        if !aliases{return Ok(c.into())}
        Ok(tx.conn().query_row("SELECT origin FROM _asset_column_renames WHERE t=?1 AND name=?2",params![t,c],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?.unwrap_or_else(||c.into()))
    };
    let fields=e.fields.iter().map(|(dest,source)|Ok((origin(child,dest)?,origin(parent,source)?))).collect::<Result<BTreeMap<_,_>,RuntimeError>>()?;
    let base=(parent,child,origin(child,&e.reference)?,fields);
    Ok(if e.each {crate::value::digest(&("each-v1",base))}else{crate::value::digest(&base)})
}
fn exists(tx: &Tx<'_>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_extraction_rows')",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}
fn load(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
    let bytes: Option<i64> = tx
        .conn()
        .query_row(
            "SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
            params![t, id],
            |r| r.get(0),
        )
        .optional()?;
    let Some(bytes) = bytes else { return Ok(None) };
    Meter::new(Ceilings::job(), Cut::Abort).heap(
        (bytes.max(0) as u64)
            .saturating_mul(64)
            .saturating_add(8192),
    )?;
    let text: String = tx.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
        params![t, id],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?;
    serde_json::from_str(&text)
        .map(Some)
        .map_err(|e| refuse(e.to_string()))
}
fn write(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(t,id) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data",params![t,id,canonical_json(row)])?;
    Ok(())
}
fn value(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    t: i64,
    id: &str,
    row: &Value,
    c: &str,
) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
    if let Ok(v) = row.field(c) {
        return Ok(Some(v.clone()));
    }
    let text: Option<String> = tx
        .conn()
        .query_row(
            "SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
            params![t, id, c],
            |r| r.get(0),
        )
        .optional()?;
    text.map(|s| serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| refuse(e.to_string())))
        .transpose()
}
fn transfer(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    v: &Value,
    from: (&str, &str, &str),
    to: (&str, &str, &str),
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if let Value::Array(vs) = v {
        for v in vs {
            transfer(tx, v, from, to)?;
        }
        return Ok(());
    }
    if v == &Value::Null {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let id = crate::assets::asset_id(Some(v))
        .ok_or_else(|| refuse("extraction asset has no typed ledger id"))?;
    let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT bytes FROM _assets WHERE id=?1 AND home_table=?2 AND home_id=?3 AND home_column=?4 AND released_at IS NULL",params![id,from.0,from.1,from.2],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
    let bytes = bytes.ok_or_else(|| {
        refuse(format!(
            "extraction asset {id} has no live source home; never claim or duplicate it"
        ))
    })?;
    let root = tx
        .meta("activation_asset_root")?
        .ok_or_else(|| refuse("extraction requires activation asset custody"))?;
    if id.is_empty()
        || !id
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_')
    {
        return Err(refuse("invalid extraction asset id"));
    }
    let path = std::path::Path::new(&root).join(id);
    let meta = std::fs::symlink_metadata(&path)
        .map_err(|_| refuse(format!("extraction asset {id} bytes are unavailable")))?;
    if !meta.is_file() || meta.len() != bytes as u64 {
        return Err(refuse(format!(
            "extraction asset {id} byte length disagrees with its ledger"
        )));
    }
    if from != to {
    tx.conn().execute(
        "UPDATE _assets SET home_table=?2,home_id=?3,home_column=?4 WHERE id=?1",
        params![id, to.0, to.1, to.2],
    )?;
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn transition(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    next: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    sources: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
    report: &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if next.extractions.is_empty() && previous.is_none_or(|s| s.extractions.is_empty()) {
        return Ok(());
    }
    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_rows(definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,child_t INTEGER NOT NULL,child_id TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(definition,parent_t,parent_id),UNIQUE(definition,child_t,child_id)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_cells(definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(definition,parent_t,parent_id,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_active(child_t INTEGER NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_name TEXT NOT NULL,reference_column TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(child_t,definition)) WITHOUT ROWID; DELETE FROM _extraction_active;")?;
    for e in &next.extractions {
        tx.conn().execute(
            "INSERT INTO _extraction_active VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4)",
            params![tx.num(&e.child)?, key(tx,e)?, e.parent, e.reference],
        )?;
    }
    tx.set_meta("version", super::table_renames::VERSION)?;
    for e in previous
        .into_iter()
        .flat_map(|s| &s.extractions)
        .filter(|e| !next.extractions.contains(e))
    {
        let Some(parent) = next.table(&e.parent) else {
            continue;
        };
        let child = &previous.unwrap().tables[&e.child];
        let pt = tx.num(&e.parent)?;
        let ct = tx.num(&e.child)?;
        let key = key(tx,e)?;
        let mut count = 0;
        let inherited = child
            .through
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|t| t.reference == e.reference && t.parent == e.parent);
        let current_parent = &previous.unwrap().tables[&e.parent];
        if parent.rules.read != crate::schema::RuleExpr::Deny
            && ((inherited && parent.rules.read != current_parent.rules.read)
                || (!inherited && parent.rules.read != child.rules.read))
        {
            return Err(refuse(
                "reverse extraction would broaden the current asset audience; preserve current read policy in an authored bridge",
            ));
        }
        for (dest, source) in &e.fields {
            if asset(&child.columns[dest])
                && parent.columns.contains_key(source)
                && parent.column_reads.get(source) != child.column_reads.get(dest)
            {
                return Err(refuse(
                    "reverse extraction would change the asset column audience; retain its current gate in a bridge",
                ));
            }
        }
        if e.each { each::backward(tx,e,parent,current_parent,child,&key,report)?; continue; }
        super::activation::walk(tx, pt, |id, mut row| {
            let saved:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT child_id FROM _extraction_rows WHERE definition=?1 AND parent_t=?2 AND parent_id=?3",params![key,pt,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
            let child_id = if let Some(id) = saved {
                id
            } else {
                let mut found = Vec::new();
                super::activation::walk(tx, ct, |child_id, child| {
                    if child.field(&e.reference) == Ok(&Value::Id(id.into())) {
                        if found.len() < 2 {
                            found.push(child_id.to_owned());
                        }
                    }
                    Ok(())
                })?;
                if found.len() > 1 {
                    return Err(refuse(format!(
                        "extraction {} parent {id} via {}: several child rows compete for {}; run rollback --check --bridge <new-dir>, keep the child population visible, choose a truthful old representation with an authorized mutation, then preview exact rollback",
                        e.child,e.reference,e.fields.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")
                    )));
                }
                let Some(child) = found.pop() else {
                    return Ok(());
                };
                child
            };
            let Some(mut image) = load(tx, ct, &child_id)? else {
                return Err(refuse(format!(
                    "extraction child {}/{child_id} was deleted; old asset has no truthful representation",
                    e.child
                )));
            };
            if image.field(&e.reference) != Ok(&Value::Id(id.into())) {
                return Err(refuse(
                    "extraction child reference changed; refusing to rebind its parent lifetime",
                ));
            }
            let anchored:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND parent_t=?4 AND parent_id=?5 AND invalid=0)",params![ct,child_id,format!("$extract:{key}"),pt,id],|r|r.get(0))?;
            if !anchored {
                return Err(refuse(
                    "extraction child lacks valid parent-lifetime evidence; refusing deletion/id reuse or retrospective recapture",
                ));
            }
            for (destination, source) in &e.fields {
                let kind = &child.columns[destination];
                if !asset(kind) {
                    if row.field(source).ok() != image.field(destination).ok() {
                        return Err(refuse(
                            "extraction immutable scalar copies disagree with the current parent",
                        ));
                    }
                    continue;
                }
                if !parent.columns.contains_key(source) {
                    continue;
                }
                let v = image.field(destination)?.clone();
                crate::engine::validate::validate_column(&e.parent,source,&parent.columns[source],Some(&v))
                    .map_err(|error|refuse(format!("reverse extraction {}.{} → {}.{source}: {error}; keep a widened bridge, repair this child through an authorized mutation, then preview exact rollback",e.child,destination,e.parent)))?;
                retain_cell(tx, &key, pt, id, source, &v)?;
                transfer(
                    tx,
                    &v,
                    (&e.child, &child_id, destination),
                    (&e.parent, id, source),
                )?;
                fields(&mut row)?.insert(source.clone(), v);
                fields(&mut image)?.remove(destination);
                tx.conn().execute(
                    "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
                    params![pt, id, source],
                )?;
            }
            write(tx, pt, id, &row)?;
            write(tx, ct, &child_id, &image)?;
            tx.conn().execute(
                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _extraction_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",
                params![key, pt, id, ct, child_id],
            )?;
            count += 1;
            Ok(())
        })?;
        report.backfills.push(format!(
            "extract {}: moved asset homes back for {count} parent rows",
            e.child
        ));
    }
    for e in next
        .extractions
        .iter()
        .filter(|e| previous.is_none_or(|s| !s.extractions.contains(e)))
    {
        e.admit(next).map_err(refuse)?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#21-wave-27-release-semantics-rel — probes retain the selected source definition's schema, including a newly declared parent.
        let (parent, source_context) = if let Some(parent) = sources.get(&e.parent) {
            (parent, sources)
        } else {
            (&next.tables[&e.parent], &next.tables)
        };
        let child = &next.tables[&e.child];
        let pt = tx.num(&e.parent)?;
        let ct = tx.num(&e.child)?;
        let key = key(tx,e)?;
        let mut count = 0;
        let inherited = child
            .through
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|t| t.reference == e.reference && t.parent == e.parent);
        let source_read = &parent.rules.read;
        if !probe_context(source_read,source_context,next) {return Err(refuse(format!("extraction {} → {}: source read rule `{}` has changed probe/index meaning; retain its typed lookup context in a bridge",e.parent,e.child,source_read.quarry())));}

        if inherited {
            if !audience_subset(&next.tables[&e.parent].rules.read,source_read) {
                return Err(refuse(
                    "asset extraction cannot broaden the inherited parent read policy; stage an explicit policy change separately",
                ));
            }
        } else if child.rules.read != crate::schema::RuleExpr::Deny {
            if !audience_subset(&child.rules.read,&mapped_audience(parent,child,e,source_read))
            {
                return Err(refuse(
                    "asset extraction requires read through its parent for row-dependent privacy; the destination cannot broaden the source audience",
                ));
            }
        }
        for (dest, source) in &e.fields {
            if let Some(parent_gate)=parent.column_reads.get(source) {
                // Immutable mapped scalar copies are the same authoritative
                // values, so a stricter child audience can name those copies.
                if !probe_context(parent_gate,source_context,next) {return Err(refuse(format!("extraction {}.{source} → {}.{dest}: source payload rule `{}` has changed probe/index meaning; retain its lookup context in a bridge",e.parent,e.child,parent_gate.quarry())));}
                let transported=mapped_audience(parent,child,e,parent_gate);
                let gate=&transported;
                let covered=child.column_reads.get(dest).is_some_and(|g|audience_subset(g,gate))
                    || (inherited&&audience_subset(&next.tables[&e.parent].rules.read,parent_gate))
                    || (!inherited&&audience_subset(&child.rules.read,gate));
                if !covered {return Err(refuse(format!("extraction {}.{source} → {}.{dest}: preserve source read rule `{}` on the child column or through its parent; an authored stricter audience is allowed",e.parent,e.child,parent_gate.quarry())));}
            }
        }
        let populated: bool =
            tx.conn()
                .query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1)", [pt], |r| {
                    r.get(0)
                })?;
        let consumed_each=e.each && !parent.columns.contains_key(each::source(e,child));
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — definition custody survives an empty parent population; every resumed child still needs its original anchor.
        let resume_each=consumed_each && each::known(tx,&key)?;
        if consumed_each && !resume_each {
            let child_history:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1)",[ct],|r|r.get(0))?;
            if populated || child_history {return Err(refuse("extract each source list is absent and no successfully applied extraction owns this suspended definition; preserve independent child history in an authored bridge"));}
        }
        for (dest, source) in e.fields.iter().filter(|_| populated) {
            if resume_each && asset(&child.columns[dest]) {continue;}
            let kind = parent.columns.get(source).ok_or_else(|| {
                refuse(format!("extraction source {}.{source} is absent", e.parent))
            })?;
            let same = if e.each && asset(&child.columns[dest]) {each::item_type(kind)==Some(&child.columns[dest])} else {kind == &child.columns[dest] || matches!(&child.columns[dest],ColumnType::Optional(inner) if inner.as_ref()==kind)};
            if !same
                || parent.engine_field(source)
                || parent.maintained.contains_key(source)
                || parent.written_by.contains_key(source)
            {
                return Err(refuse(
                    format!("extraction {}.{source} → {}.{dest} requires identical authoritative types or required → optional widening",e.parent,e.child),
                ));
            }
            if asset(kind) && next.tables[&e.parent].columns.contains_key(source) {
                return Err(refuse(
                    "extraction must remove the source asset column; one asset cannot have two active homes",
                ));
            }
            if !asset(kind)&&(!parent.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,crate::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(source)))||matches!(kind,ColumnType::Ref{..}|ColumnType::Optional(_))){return Err(refuse("extraction scalar copies require immutable source fields without reference/captured authority"))}
        }
        if e.each { if resume_each {each::resume(tx,e,parent,child,&key,report)?;}else{each::forward(tx,e,parent,child,&key,report)?;} continue; }
        super::activation::walk(tx, pt, |id, mut row| {
            let saved:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT child_id FROM _extraction_rows WHERE definition=?1 AND parent_t=?2 AND parent_id=?3",params![key,pt,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
            let child_id = saved.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| id.to_owned());
            let present = load(tx, ct, &child_id)?;
            let independent:bool=if saved.is_none(){tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _extraction_rows WHERE child_t=?1 AND child_id=?2)",params![ct,child_id],|r|r.get(0))?}else{false};
            if saved.is_none() && (present.is_some()||independent) {
                return Err(refuse(format!(
                    "extraction destination {}/{child_id} already has independent history",
                    e.child
                )));
            }
            if saved.is_some(){
                let anchored:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND parent_t=?4 AND parent_id=?5 AND invalid=0)",params![ct,child_id,format!("$extract:{key}"),pt,id],|r|r.get(0))?;
                if present.is_none()||!anchored{return Err(refuse("returning extraction lacks its receipt-owned child or valid parent-lifetime evidence; never recreate pruned/deleted history"))}
            }
            let mut image = present.unwrap_or_else(|| {
                Value::Row(
                    BTreeMap::from([
                        ("id".into(), Value::Id(child_id.clone())),
                        (e.reference.clone(), Value::Id(id.into())),
                    ])
                    .into(),
                )
            });
            if image.field(&e.reference) != Ok(&Value::Id(id.into())) {
                return Err(refuse("extraction cannot rebind a child parent"));
            }
            for (destination, source) in &e.fields {
                let kind = &child.columns[destination];
                let Some(v) = value(tx, pt, id, &row, source)? else {
                    continue;
                };
                if asset(kind) {
                    if image.field(destination).is_ok_and(|old| old != &v && !(saved.is_some() && old == &Value::Null)) {
                        return Err(refuse(
                            "extraction destination asset conflicts with its source",
                        ));
                    }
                    retain_cell(tx, &key, pt, id, source, &v)?;
                    transfer(
                        tx,
                        &v,
                        (&e.parent, id, source),
                        (&e.child, &child_id, destination),
                    )?;
                    fields(&mut image)?.insert(destination.clone(), v);
                    fields(&mut row)?.remove(source);
                    tx.conn().execute(
                        "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
                        params![pt, id, source],
                    )?;
                } else if saved.is_none() {
                    fields(&mut image)?.insert(destination.clone(), v);
                } else if image.field(destination).ok() != Some(&v) {
                    return Err(refuse(
                        "extraction immutable scalar copies disagree with the returning parent",
                    ));
                }
            }
            crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut image, child);
            write(tx, pt, id, &row)?;
            write(tx, ct, &child_id, &image)?;
            tx.conn().execute(
                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _extraction_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",
                params![key, pt, id, ct, child_id],
            )?;
            super::dormant::capture_refs(
                tx,
                ct,
                &child_id,
                &format!("$extract:{key}"),
                &ColumnType::Ref {
                    table: e.parent.clone(),
                },
                &Value::Id(id.into()),
            )?;
            count += 1;
            Ok(())
        })?;
        report.backfills.push(format!(
            "extract {}: prepared {count} child rows and moved existing asset homes",
            e.child
        ));
    }
    tx.set_meta("activation_population", "true")?;
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn delete(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if exists(tx)? {
        each::delete(tx,t,id)?;
        tx.conn().execute(
            "DELETE FROM _extraction_cells WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2",
            params![t, id],
        )?;
        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _extraction_rows WHERE (parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2) OR (child_t=?1 AND child_id=?2)",params![t,id])?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn observe_write(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    t: i64,
    id: &str,
    row: &Value,
    old: Option<&Value>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if !exists(tx)? {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let active:Vec<(String,String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition,parent_name,reference_column FROM _extraction_active WHERE child_t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    for (key, parent, column) in active {
        let binding = format!("$extract:{key}");
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — only birth or a real reference change can capture a parent lifetime; an asset edit cannot repair missing provenance.
        if old.is_some_and(|old| old.field(&column).ok() == row.field(&column).ok()) {
            continue;
        }
        if let Ok(value) = row.field(&column) {
            super::dormant::capture_refs(
                tx,
                t,
                id,
                &binding,
                &ColumnType::Ref { table: parent },
                value,
            )?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn retain_cell(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    key: &str,
    t: i64,
    id: &str,
    column: &str,
    value: &Value,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let old: Option<String> = tx
        .conn()
        .query_row(
            "SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
            params![t, id, column],
            |r| r.get(0),
        )
        .optional()?;
    if let Some(old) = old {
        let old: Value = serde_json::from_str(&old).map_err(|e| refuse(e.to_string()))?;
        if &old != value {
            return Err(refuse(
                "extraction has a distinct retained source cell; preserve both meanings in an authored bridge",
            ));
        }
    }
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _extraction_cells VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5) ON CONFLICT(definition,parent_t,parent_id,column_name) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data",params![key,t,id,column,canonical_json(value)])?;
    Ok(())
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
//! A family command expands to current individual, source-bound records.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#18-wave-26-security-decisions-dx9 — review questions lead, and gates never enter the audience family.
use super::*;
use snapback4_lang::Diagnostic;

pub(super) fn audience(code: &str) -> bool {
    !gated(code) && matches!(code, "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE" | "N_BROAD_READ")
}
pub(super) fn family(notes: &[Diagnostic], identities: &[(String,String,String)]) -> Option<String> {
    let mut members = notes.iter().zip(identities).filter(|(note,_)| audience(&note.code))
        .map(|(_, (id,source,_))| (id,source)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if members.is_empty() { return None; }
    members.sort();
    Some(format!("audience:{}", hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&(ANALYZER,members)).unwrap())))
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn count(dispositions: &[Disposition]) -> usize {
    let audience = dispositions.iter().any(|d| !d.acknowledged && d.family.is_some());
    usize::from(audience) + dispositions.iter().filter(|d| !d.acknowledged && d.family.is_none()
        && crate::note_rank::tier(&d.code)==crate::note_rank::Tier::Review).count()
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn print(dispositions: &[Disposition], notes: &[Diagnostic]) {print!("{}",render(dispositions,notes));}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn render(dispositions: &[Disposition], notes: &[Diagnostic]) -> String {
    use std::fmt::Write;
    let mut output=String::new();
    let mut ordered=notes.iter().zip(dispositions).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#165-review-clarification-exact-detector-and-command-boundaries — grouping must preserve gate/authority priority.
    ordered.sort_by_key(|(n,_)|(crate::note_rank::summary_order(&n.code),n.site.file.clone(),n.site.line,n.site.col));
    let members=ordered.iter().filter(|(_,d)|d.family.is_some()&&!d.acknowledged).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut family_output=String::new();
    if let Some((_,first))=members.first().copied() {
        let codes=members.iter().map(|(n,_)|n.code.as_str()).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        writeln!(&mut family_output,"decision audience ({codes}): Are these {} row/column audiences, roles and lifecycle states intended?",members.len()).unwrap();
        for (n,_) in &members {
            // The expanded branch cross-product stays in JSON. One question
            // names the source subject without repeating every equivalent path.
            let subject = n.message.split(" matches or weakens").next().unwrap_or(&n.message)
                .split(": ").next().unwrap_or(&n.message);
            writeln!(&mut family_output,"  ? {}: Is {} intended?",n.site,subject.trim_end_matches('.')).unwrap();
        }
        writeln!(&mut family_output,"  Intended: snapback4 check --ack-security {} --reason '<spec rationale>'",first.family.as_deref().unwrap()).unwrap();
        writeln!(&mut family_output,"  Narrower: restrict the listed rules; declare independent intent; run snapback4 test. Full audiences: check --json.").unwrap();
    }
    let mut family_printed=false;
    for (n,d) in ordered {
        if d.acknowledged { writeln!(&mut output,"security reviewed {} {}: {}",d.id,d.code,d.reason.as_deref().unwrap_or("")).unwrap(); continue; }
        if d.family.is_some() {
            if !family_printed {output.push_str(&family_output);family_printed=true;}
            continue;
        }
        if crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review { continue; }
        let subject=n.message.split(": ").next().unwrap_or(&n.message);
        let concise=match n.code.as_str() {
            "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE"=>format!("{subject}: should operation results/refusals expose protected or projected-out columns to every admitted caller"),
            _=>n.message.trim_end_matches(['.', '?']).to_owned(),
        };
        let question=concise.trim_end_matches(['.', '?']);
        writeln!(&mut output,"decision {} at {}: {question}; is this intended?",d.code,d.site).unwrap();
        let repair=match n.code.as_str() {
            "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE"=>"restrict result/refusal audiences with column rules and independent intent; run snapback4 test",
            "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT"=>"validate the recipient's current role and relationship; caller authority alone is insufficient",
            _=>n.rewrite.as_str(),
        };
        writeln!(&mut output,"  Repair: {repair}").unwrap();
        writeln!(&mut output,"  Intended: snapback4 check --ack-security {} --reason '<spec rationale>'{}",d.id,if d.gated {" (required before check can pass)"}else{""}).unwrap();
    }
    output
}

//! Owner-mediated release publication in the running HTTP process.
use super::*;
use crate::release_control::Command;
#[cfg(unix)]
use crate::release_control::Listener;
use snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation;

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(super) fn start(
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    dir: std::path::PathBuf,
) -> Result<std::thread::JoinHandle<()>, String> {
    let store = shared
        .owner
        .as_ref()
        .ok_or("release control requires a persisted owner")?
        .path()
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let listener = Listener::bind(&dir, store)?;
    crate::lifecycle::managed_cleanup();
    crate::lifecycle::background("release-control", move || {
        listener.run(|command| apply(&shared, &dir, command))
    })
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — only assignments and committed rebind follow the database commit.
pub(super) fn apply(
    shared: &Shared,
    dir: &std::path::Path,
    command: Command,
) -> Result<Json, String> {
    match command {
        Command::Stop => {
            crate::lifecycle::request_stop();
            Ok(json!({"stopping":true}))
        }
        Command::Status {
            after,
            items_after,
            event,
        } => {
            shared.engine.attempt(move |engine, shared| {
            let mut status=engine.store_mut().release_status(after,items_after.as_deref(),event).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            status["clock"]=json!({"now":shared.now(),"offsetMs":lock(&shared.clock).offset()});
            Ok(status)
            })
        },
        Command::Prune {
            confirm,
            expected_revision,
        } => {
            let mut result = shared.engine.attempt(move |engine, _| engine.store_mut().prune_dormant(confirm, Some(expected_revision)))
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if let Some(items) = result["items"].as_array_mut() {
                items.truncate(16);
            }
            result["items_after"] = result["items"]
                .as_array()
                .and_then(|items| items.last())
                .map(|i| i["key"].clone())
                .unwrap_or(Json::Null);
            Ok(result)
        }
        Command::Activate {
            activation_protocol,
            release,
            expected_release,
            expected_revision,
            digest,
            rollback,
            visible_rollback,
            registry_custody,
            restore_dormant,
            allow_feed_adoption,
        } => {
            if activation_protocol != crate::release_control::ACTIVATION_PROTOCOL {
                return Err("release activation protocol differs; restart serve with this CLI before switching releases".into());
            }
            if release == 0 {
                return Err("release id must be positive".into());
            }
            let project = Project {
                root: dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string()),
            };
            if !std::fs::symlink_metadata(&project.root)
                .is_ok_and(|m| m.is_dir() && !m.file_type().is_symlink())
            {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release {release} is not an immutable release directory"
                ));
            }
            if !registry_custody{return Err("release custody requires an updated release-control client; use this runtime's CLI".into())}
            crate::verbs::validate_registered_view(dir,&project.root)?;
            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
                return Err("staged release content changed; redeploy its exact source".into());
            }
            if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release {release} has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release"
                ));
            }
            let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
                return Err(bound
                    .diagnostics
                    .iter()
                    .map(ToString::to_string)
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join("\n"));
            }
            let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
            crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
                return Err("release artifacts changed during preparation; retry after inspecting immutable source".into());
            }
            crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
            let mut pointer =
                crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, expected_release, rollback)?;
            let options = ReleaseActivation {
                visibility:crate::verbs::visibility_policy(&project.root)?.or_else(||visible_rollback.then(Default::default)),
                release,
                expected_previous: expected_release,
                expected_revision: Some(expected_revision),
                restore_dormant,
                native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
                operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
            };
            let _requests=shared.release_requests.write();
            shared.engine.attempt(move |engine, shared| {
            lock(&shared.readers).begin_adoption(&bound);
            let generation = match engine.adopt_release_at(
                bound.schema.clone(),
                bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
                &options,
                allow_feed_adoption,
                shared.now(),
            ) {
                Ok(generation) => generation,
                Err(error) => {
                    lock(&shared.readers).finish_adoption();
                    return Err(error.to_string());
                }
            };
            #[cfg(test)]
            test_work(&shared.release_committed);
            engine.set_prepared_native(prepared.module);
            engine.set_presence(Some(shared.presence.clone()));
            shared.refresh_time_audience_clock(engine, shared.now());
            shared.replace_readers(engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
            {
                let mut active = lock(&shared.bound);
                let mut advertised = lock(&shared.generation);
                let mut native = lock(&shared.native);
                shared.latency.adopt(&bound);
                *active = bound;
                *advertised = generation;
                *native = prepared.installed;
                let mut root = lock(&shared.release_root);
                *root = Some(project.root);
                shared.worker_revision.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Release);
            }
            crate::lifecycle::generation(generation);
            shared.publish_engine(engine);
            // Completion I/O can fail, but never abandons the new runtime.
            pointer.finish(generation)?;
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
                "snapback4 serve: release {release} activated without restart as generation {generation}"
            );
            Ok(
                json!({"release":release,"generation":generation,"revision":expected_revision+1,"hot":true,"adoption":engine.store().adoption_report().compact(),"details":engine.store().adoption_report().summary(),"backfills":engine.store().adoption_report().backfills}),
            )
            })
        }
    }
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
import { synopsis } from "./cli-usage.js";
// `npx snapback4 <verb>`: the Rust binary, found in the platform package
// installed beside this one, or named by SNAPBACK4_BIN, or built in this
// repository. The JavaScript here only finds and runs it, and the two live and
// die together: SIGINT, SIGTERM and SIGHUP to this wrapper are forwarded, and
// SIGKILL, which cannot be forwarded, still stops the binary: it is told this
// wrapper's PID (SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID) and exits when its parent is gone
// (Linux: the kernel's parent-death signal; elsewhere within 250 ms). So a
// recorded PID of either stops the server; `GET /dev/status` reports both
// (`pid` is the binary, `wrapperPid` this wrapper), and so does the stderr line
// `SNAPBACK4_READY … url=… pid=… wrapperPid=…`.
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { resolveBinary } from "../dist/resolve-binary.js";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { constants } from "node:os";
import { firstCheckWarnings } from "./upgrade-warnings.js";

if (process.argv[2] === "check" && !process.argv.includes("--help")) firstCheckWarnings();

if (process.argv[2] === "web-shell") {
  try { await (await import("./web-shell.js")).runWebShell(process.argv.slice(3)); }
  catch (error) { console.error(`E_INPUT at web-shell: ${error.message}\n  rewrite: export a static app, then run snapback4 web-shell dist --base /`); process.exit(2); }
  process.exit(0);
}

if (process.argv[2] === "serve-check") {
  try {
    const args = process.argv.slice(3), options = {};
    if (args.includes("--help")) {
      console.log(`usage: ${synopsis("serve-check")}`);
      process.exit(0);
    }
    const keys = {"--deployment":"deployment", "--script":"script", "--up-ms":"upMs", "--timeout-ms":"timeoutMs"};
    for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 2) {
      const key = keys[args[i]];
      if (!key || args[i + 1] === undefined) throw Error("unknown/missing serve-check option; see --help");
      options[key] = key.endsWith("Ms") ? Number(args[i + 1]) : args[i + 1];
    }
    const {serveCheck} = await import("../dist/proof-serve-check.js");
    const report = await serveCheck(options);
    console.log(JSON.stringify(report)); process.exitCode = report.ok ? 0 : 1;
  } catch (error) { console.error(`E_SERVE_CHECK: ${error.message}`); process.exitCode = 2; }
} else {
let binary;
try { binary = resolveBinary(); }
catch (error) { console.error(error.message); process.exit(2); }
// Internal export helpers share the wrapper's kernel lease through fd 3.
const exportLease = process.argv[2] === "witness" && ["export-lease", "export-publish"].includes(process.argv[3]);
const child = spawn(binary, process.argv.slice(2), { stdio: exportLease ? ["inherit", "inherit", "inherit", 3] : "inherit", env: {
  ...process.env, SNAPBACK4_ACQUIRE_HELPER: fileURLToPath(new URL("./witness-acquire.js", import.meta.url)),
  SNAPBACK4_UPGRADE_HELPER: fileURLToPath(new URL("./upgrade.js", import.meta.url)),
  SNAPBACK4_WEB_HELPER: fileURLToPath(new URL("./witness-web.js", import.meta.url)),
  SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID: String(process.pid),
} });
const handlers = new Map(["SIGINT", "SIGTERM", "SIGHUP"].map(signal => [signal, () => { try { child.kill(signal); } catch { /* gone */ } }]));
for (const [signal, handler] of handlers) process.on(signal, handler);
// `npx snapback4 dev` puts npm and `sh -c` above this wrapper. A SIGTERM to the
// npx PID kills npm and the shell but never reaches us, so the server would keep
// serving as an orphan. Our parent's death is the stop signal. (`process.ppid` is
// read once at startup, so probe the original parent instead.)
const parent = process.ppid;
const parentAlive = () => { try { process.kill(parent, 0); return true; } catch (error) { return error.code === "EPERM"; } };
const orphanWatch = parent > 1 ? setInterval(() => {
  if (!parentAlive()) { try { child.kill("SIGTERM"); } catch { /* gone */ } }
}, 250) : null;
orphanWatch?.unref();
child.on("exit", (code, signal) => {
  if (orphanWatch) clearInterval(orphanWatch);
  for (const [name, handler] of handlers) process.off(name, handler);
  if (signal) {
    // Restore the default disposition before re-raising. Some platforms do not
    // terminate on this call; never let their empty event loop report success.
    const status = 128 + (constants.signals[signal] ?? { SIGHUP: 1, SIGINT: 2, SIGTERM: 15 }[signal] ?? 1);
    setTimeout(() => process.exit(status), 100);
    try { process.kill(process.pid, signal); } catch { process.exit(status); }
    return;
  }
  process.exit(code ?? 1);
});

}

 succeeded in 0ms:
//! `check`'s advisory notes, ranked. A green check printed nine notes in
//! source order, and builders could not tell an FYI about offline coverage
//! from a page that never completes. Notes stay advisory (they never fail a
//! check); security review gates are decided by the caller. This module orders
//! output, names each group and shortens repeats.

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Debug)]
pub(crate) enum Tier {
    /// Can cost correctness or completeness: a page that never completes, a
    /// write no caller can pass, a disclosure, a read a device cannot answer.
    Review,
    /// Costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage.
    Performance,
    /// What a declaration implies; nothing to fix for online use.
    Design,
}

pub(crate) fn tier(code: &str) -> Tier {
    if snapback4_lang::security::gated_note(code)||snapback4_lang::security::note_priority(code)<=2{return Tier::Review;}
    match code {
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" | "N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL" | "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" | "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" | "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" | "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" | "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" | "N_WRITE_DENIED" | "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" | "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" | "N_PATCH_CLEARS" | "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" | "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_VACUOUS_RULE" | "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED" | "N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" | "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" | "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" | "N_QUERY_FILTERED" | "N_SHAPE_METER" | "N_HELD_UNKNOWN" | "N_SEQUENCE_READ"
        | "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" | "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" | "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"
        | "N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" | "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE" | "N_SEARCH_UNSCOPED" | "N_SEARCH_OPEN" | "N_TIMELINE_PROFILE_CAP" | "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" | "N_SELF_CLAIMED_RELATIONSHIP" | "N_CALLER_TIME" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT" | "N_BROAD_READ" | "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => Tier::Review,
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.4 — a cursorless `first N` is what the declaration says: the first N rows (wave 3 #7).
        "N_SYNC_ORDER" | "N_NO_HORIZON" | "N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED" | "N_TIMELINE_COMPAT" | "N_NO_CURSOR" => Tier::Design,
        _ => Tier::Performance,
    }
}

/// Security findings that can grant authority stay ahead of repeated advice.
pub(crate) fn summary_order(code:&str)->(Tier,u8){
    let priority=snapback4_lang::security::note_priority(code);
    (if priority<=2 {Tier::Review}else{tier(code)},priority)
}

pub(crate) fn site_order(site:&str)->(String,u32,u32){
    let mut parts=site.rsplitn(3,':');let col=parts.next().and_then(|s|s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);let line=parts.next().and_then(|s|s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);let file=parts.next().unwrap_or(site);(file.to_owned(),line,col)
}

/// The first words of a note's line: what to do about it, or that nothing
/// needs doing (wave 7 #11: "put a one-line verdict on every advisory note").
// @ref LLP 3000.036#17.7 — the verdict leads; the explanation follows it.
pub(crate) fn verdict(code: &str, summary: &str, form: bool) -> &'static str {
    match code {
        "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" => "review: restrict authority assignment",
        "N_SELF_CLAIMED_RELATIONSHIP" => "review: verify the relationship before granting access",
        "N_CALLER_TIME" => "review: use server time or bound the supplied time",
        "N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_BROAD_READ" => "review: confirm each admitted audience and row state",
        "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" => "review: answer the audience questions from the spec",
        "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => "review: write independent intent from the spec",
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" if summary.starts_with("persona-only:") => "no action without use identity",
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" => "fix: add a production bootstrap writer",
        "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" => "fix: update the fixture arguments",
        "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" => "review: complete for the pinned parent audience; other callers may be capped",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("the complete form is to add `cap ") => "fix: add the exact source cap below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("no verified complete form for this read") => "review: no verified complete form for this read; expect `capped` and show what arrived",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form && summary.contains("the closest form is") => "fix: use the closest form below (one arm stays `complete: false`), or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form => "fix: use the complete form below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" => "fix: pin the scope, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" => "fix: declare the reference's `on delete`, or guard the delete",
        "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" => "review: some deleters get NOT_FOUND",
        "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" => "review: this reference argument has no mandatory target lookup",
        "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" => "review: hidden and absent are different caller outcomes",
        "N_PATCH_CLEARS" => "review: an omitted argument clears this column",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" if summary.contains("admits only 'service:") => "review: a seat require grants no table authority",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" => "fix: this write refuses E_RULE for callers you admit",
        "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" => "review: callers you admit may still get E_RULE",
        "N_QUERY_FILTERED" => "review: this query branch may filter rows under table authority",
        "N_READ_DENIED" => "fix: this read always returns null",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" if summary.contains("get NOT_FOUND") => "review: the hidden lookup runs before this require",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" => "fix: this require cannot see the rows it tests",
        "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" => "fix: this nullable lookup is always null for the admitted callers",
        "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" => "review: a required read gate may hide rows from writers",
        "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED" => "review: confirm lookup authority and the hidden-row outcome",
        "N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN" => "review: hidden unique conflicts retain generic E_RULE",
        "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" => "review: who can read this",
        "N_VACUOUS_RULE" => "review: who can write this",
        "N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" => "review: confirm creation authority as well as edit authority",
        "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE" => "review: confirm every result/refusal audience",
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" => "review: readable conflicts require the original immutable value",
        "N_NO_CURSOR" => "no action for a top-N list",
        _ => match tier(code) {
            Tier::Review => "review",
            Tier::Performance => "no action unless it is slow (`check --cost` measures it)",
            Tier::Design => "no action for an online app",
        },
    }
}

fn heading(tier: Tier) -> &'static str {
    match tier {
        Tier::Review => "review — can cost correctness or completeness:",
        Tier::Performance => "performance — costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage:",
        Tier::Design => "by design — what a declaration implies; no action needed for online use:",
    }
}

pub(crate) struct Note {
    pub site: String,
    pub code: String,
    pub summary: String,
    /// An expanded compiled repair: its lead line and the code. Compact
    /// completeness notes leave this empty and keep the repair in `--verbose`.
    pub form: Option<(String, String)>,
    /// Three-line default rendering for completeness verdicts. Their full
    /// planner proof and compiled repair remain in `check --verbose`.
    pub compact: Option<[String; 3]>,
}

/// The compiled form leading a note's rewrite: the text before "Otherwise:",
/// when it holds code (a lead line ending in `:` and the declaration under it).
// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.5 — the compiled owner-or-X rewrite prints by default, once per form.
pub(crate) fn compiled_form(code: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    if !matches!(code, "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE") { return None; }
    let head = rewrite.split("\n\nOtherwise:").next()?;
    let (lead, body) = head.split_once('\n')?;
    (lead.ends_with(':') && body.trim_start().starts_with("query ")).then(|| (lead.to_owned(), body.trim_end().to_owned()))
}

// Audience questions and repairs differ per rule; repeated codes cannot hide them.
fn per_rule_security(code:&str)->bool {snapback4_lang::security::note_priority(code)<=2||matches!(code,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"|"N_SELF_CLAIMED_RELATIONSHIP"|"N_CALLER_TIME"|"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"|"N_BROAD_READ"|"N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE")}

/// The ranked block printed after `check: ok`; empty for no notes.
pub(crate) fn render(notes: Vec<Note>) -> Vec<String> {
    if notes.is_empty() { return Vec::new(); }
    // Collapse one code's repeats at one site, keeping first-seen order.
    let mut unique: Vec<(Note, usize)> = Vec::new();
    for note in notes {
        match unique.iter_mut().find(|(seen, _)| seen.site == note.site && seen.code == note.code && seen.summary == note.summary && seen.form == note.form && seen.compact == note.compact) {
            Some((_, count)) => *count += 1,
            None => unique.push((note, 1)),
        }
    }
    let total = unique.len();
    // Rank by tier; within a tier, a code's notes stay together in the order the code first appeared.
    let mut order: Vec<usize> = (0..unique.len()).collect();
    order.sort_by_key(|&i| (summary_order(&unique[i].0.code), site_order(&unique[i].0.site), i));
    let mut lines = vec![format!("{total} thing{} to review (advisory; check --verbose explains each, snapback4 why <CODE> one):",
        if total == 1 { "" } else { "s" })];
    let mut current = None;
    let mut shown_codes = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_forms = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_compacts = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_actions = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_context = Vec::new();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#16.5 — wave 6 #25: a code's repeats share one line naming each subject.
    let mut repeats: Vec<(String, Vec<String>)> = Vec::new();
    let flush = |lines: &mut Vec<String>, repeats: &mut Vec<(String, Vec<String>)>| {
        for (code, subjects) in repeats.drain(..) {
            lines.push(format!("note: {code} (as above) also for {}", subjects.join("; ")));
        }
    };
    for i in order {
        let (note, count) = &unique[i];
        let group = tier(&note.code);
        if current != Some(group) { flush(&mut lines, &mut repeats); lines.push(heading(group).to_owned()); current = Some(group); }
        if note.code == "N_NO_CURSOR" {
            if !shown_codes.contains(&note.code) {
                let subjects = unique.iter().filter(|(n,_)| n.code == "N_NO_CURSOR").map(|(n,_)|
                    format!("{} ({})", subject(&n.summary), n.site)).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ");
                lines.push(format!("note: N_NO_CURSOR: no action for a top-N list. Pages without continuation: {subjects}; if the screen pages, add `c: cursor ?` and `after c`"));
                shown_codes.push(note.code.clone());
            }
            continue;
        }
        let times = if *count > 1 { format!(" (×{count} at this site)") } else { String::new() };
        let new_form = note.form.as_ref().is_some_and(|(_, code)| !shown_forms.contains(code));
        let new_compact = note.compact.as_ref().is_some_and(|parts| !shown_compacts.contains(parts));
        if shown_codes.contains(&note.code) && !new_form && !new_compact && !per_rule_security(&note.code) {
            // A repeat of a code already explained above: its subject and site, on the code's one line.
            let subject = format!("{} ({}){times}", subject(&note.summary), note.site);
            match repeats.iter_mut().find(|(code, _)| *code == note.code) {
                Some((_, subjects)) => subjects.push(subject),
                None => repeats.push((note.code.clone(), vec![subject])),
            }
            continue;
        }
        flush(&mut lines, &mut repeats);
        if let Some(parts @ [action, impact, audience]) = &note.compact {
            if !shown_actions.contains(action) {
                let mut body = action.lines();
                lines.push(format!("note: {} — {} — {}{times}", note.site, note.code, body.next().unwrap_or("review")));
                lines.extend(body.map(str::to_owned));
                shown_actions.push(action.clone());
            } else {
                lines.push(format!("note: {} — {} — {}: checked rewrite or limitation as above{times}", note.site, note.code, subject(&note.summary)));
            }
            for context in [impact, audience] {
                if !shown_context.contains(&(note.code.clone(), context.clone())) {
                    lines.push(format!("  {context}"));
                    shown_context.push((note.code.clone(), context.clone()));
                }
            }
            shown_compacts.push(parts.clone());
            shown_codes.push(note.code.clone());
            continue;
        }
        if shown_codes.contains(&note.code) && !per_rule_security(&note.code) {
            // A repeat that carries a form not printed yet keeps its own line, then the form.
            lines.push(format!("note: {} — {}: (as above) {}{times}", note.site, note.code, short(&note.summary)));
        } else {
            let below = note.form.as_ref().is_some_and(|(_, code)| !shown_forms.contains(code));
            let summary = match note.summary.strip_suffix(" (check --verbose)") { Some(text) if below => format!("{text} (below)"), _ => note.summary.clone() };
            lines.push(format!("note: {} — {}: {}. {summary}{times}", note.site, note.code, verdict(&note.code, &note.summary, below)));
            shown_codes.push(note.code.clone());
        }
        if let Some((lead, code)) = &note.form && !shown_forms.contains(code) {
            lines.push(format!("  rewrite: {lead}"));
            lines.extend(code.lines().map(|line| format!("    {line}")));
            shown_forms.push(code.clone());
        }
    }
    flush(&mut lines, &mut repeats);
    // Every advisory line has a direct route to the acceptance recipe.
    for line in &mut lines {
        if let Some(code) = line.split_whitespace().find(|word| word.starts_with("N_")) {
            let code = code.trim_end_matches(':').to_owned();
            line.push_str(&format!(" — snapback4 why {code}"));
        }
    }
    lines
}

/// The operation or table a repeat is about (`query b`), else its short subject.
fn subject(summary: &str) -> String {
    let mut words = summary.splitn(3, ' ');
    match (words.next(), words.next()) {
        (Some(kind @ ("query" | "mutation" | "job" | "action" | "table" | "view")), Some(name)) => format!("{kind} {}", name.trim_end_matches(':')),
        _ => short(summary),
    }
}

/// The subject of a repeated note: its first clause before any quoted source, at most 100 characters.
fn short(summary: &str) -> String {
    let clause = summary.split("; ").next().unwrap_or(summary);
    // The quoted source is above already; the subject before it names the repeat.
    let clause = clause.split_once(" `").map_or(clause, |(subject, _)| subject).trim_end_matches(':');
    match clause.char_indices().nth(100) {
        Some((at, _)) => format!("{}…", &clause[..at]),
        None => clause.to_owned(),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn note(site: &str, code: &str, summary: &str) -> Note { Note { site: site.into(), code: code.into(), summary: summary.into(), form: None, compact: None } }

    #[test]
    fn security_priority_and_numeric_site_order_survive_rendering() {
        let text=render(vec![note("schema.q:101:1","N_ROLE_WIDE_READ","later audience"),note("schema.q:50:1","N_CREATION_REASON","middle reason"),note("schema.q:41:1","N_ROLE_WIDE_READ","first audience"),note("schema.q:1:1","N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE","inventory")]).join("\n");
        assert!(text.find("first audience")<text.find("middle reason"));
        assert!(text.find("middle reason")<text.find("later audience"));
        assert!(text.find("later audience")<text.find("inventory"));
        for code in ["N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT","N_CREATION_REASON"]{assert_eq!(tier(code),Tier::Review);assert!(!verdict(code,"",false).contains("slow"));}
    }

    #[test]
    fn repeated_security_codes_keep_each_rules_audience_and_question() {
        let text=render(vec![note("schema.q:2:1","N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE","notes.read: should coach see other athletes?"),note("schema.q:8:1","N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE","pieces.address: should intake see every owner address?")]).join("\n");
        assert!(text.contains("should coach see other athletes?")&&text.contains("should intake see every owner address?"),"{text}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_compiled_complete_form_prints_once_per_lead_by_default() {
        let rewrite = "Branch on the grant and pin the owner for everyone else; both pages are complete:\nquery q(c: cursor ?):\n  if exists staff[viewer]:\n    return t last 50 by .at after c\n  return t where .owner = viewer last 50 by .at after c\n\nOtherwise: pin .owner";
        let form = compiled_form("N_NEVER_COMPLETE", rewrite);
        assert_eq!(form.as_ref().map(|(lead, _)| lead.as_str()), Some("Branch on the grant and pin the owner for everyone else; both pages are complete:"));
        assert!(compiled_form("N_NEVER_COMPLETE", "pin .owner with where equalities").is_none());
        assert!(compiled_form("N_SYNC_ORDER", rewrite).is_none());
        let with = |site: &str| Note { form: form.clone(), ..note(site, "N_NEVER_COMPLETE", "query q: never complete") };
        let lines = render(vec![with("schema.q:1:1"), with("schema.q:9:1")]);
        assert_eq!(lines[2..8], [
            "note: schema.q:1:1 — N_NEVER_COMPLETE: fix: use the complete form below, or accept `complete: false`. query q: never complete — snapback4 why N_NEVER_COMPLETE".to_owned(),
            "  rewrite: Branch on the grant and pin the owner for everyone else; both pages are complete:".into(),
            "    query q(c: cursor ?):".into(),
            "      if exists staff[viewer]:".into(),
            "        return t last 50 by .at after c".into(),
            "      return t where .owner = viewer last 50 by .at after c".into(),
        ], "{lines:#?}");
        assert_eq!(lines.len(), 9, "the second note of the same form is the short repeat: {lines:#?}");
        assert_eq!(lines[8], "note: N_NEVER_COMPLETE (as above) also for query q (schema.q:9:1) — snapback4 why N_NEVER_COMPLETE", "{lines:#?}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn distinct_scans_at_one_site_and_distinct_query_repairs_remain_visible() {
        let n = |subject: &str, repair: &str| Note { compact: Some([repair.into(), "impact".into(), "audience".into()]), ..note("s.q:1:1", "N_NEVER_COMPLETE", subject) };
        let lines = render(vec![n("query q: scan a", "rewrite a"), n("query q: scan b", "rewrite b"), n("query r", "rewrite r"), n("query r", "rewrite r")]);
        let output = lines.join("\n");
        for repair in ["rewrite a", "rewrite b", "rewrite r"] { assert_eq!(output.matches(repair).count(), 1, "{output}"); }
        assert!(output.contains("×2 at this site"), "{output}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn identical_actions_keep_different_scan_audiences() {
        let with = |audience: &str| Note { compact: Some(["review query both: missing pin .owner".into(), "same impact".into(), audience.into()]), ..note("s.q:1:1", "N_NEVER_COMPLETE", "query both") };
        let output = render(vec![with("staff are complete (scan tickets)"), with("tutors are complete (scan papers)")]).join("\n");
        assert!(output.contains("staff are complete (scan tickets)"), "{output}");
        assert!(output.contains("tutors are complete (scan papers)"), "{output}");
        assert_eq!(output.matches("same impact").count(), 1, "{output}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn completeness_note_is_three_lines_and_points_to_verbose_detail() {
        let compact = Some([
            "verdict: capped; completeness is not proved".into(),
            "who: no caller group is proved complete by this page".into(),
            "what: pin the scope; check --verbose shows the checked repair".into(),
        ]);
        let lines = render(vec![Note { compact, ..note("schema.q:8:1", "N_NEVER_COMPLETE", "long planner proof") }]);
        assert_eq!(&lines[2..], [
            "note: schema.q:8:1 — N_NEVER_COMPLETE — verdict: capped; completeness is not proved — snapback4 why N_NEVER_COMPLETE",
            "  who: no caller group is proved complete by this page",
            "  what: pin the scope; check --verbose shows the checked repair",
        ]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn notes_rank_by_consequence_and_collapse_repeats() {
        let lines = render(vec![
            note("schema.q:95:1", "N_WIDE_MERGE", "query feed: read cost grows"),
            note("schema.q:71:1", "N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED", "2 synced maintains"),
            note("schema.q:78:10", "N_SYNC_ORDER", "query a reads by .id; the client needs the server"),
            note("schema.q:87:10", "N_SYNC_ORDER", "query b `t first 5 by .at` reads by .at; the client needs the server"),
            note("schema.q:104:1", "N_WRITE_DENIED", "mutation rangers inserts rows no caller may insert"),
            note("schema.q:104:1", "N_WRITE_DENIED", "mutation rangers inserts rows no caller may insert"),
        ]);
        assert!(lines[0].starts_with("5 things to review"), "{lines:?}");
        let body: Vec<&str> = lines[1..].iter().map(String::as_str).collect();
        assert_eq!(body, [
            "review — can cost correctness or completeness:",
            "note: schema.q:104:1 — N_WRITE_DENIED: fix: this write refuses E_RULE for callers you admit. mutation rangers inserts rows no caller may insert (×2 at this site) — snapback4 why N_WRITE_DENIED",
            "performance — costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage:",
            "note: schema.q:95:1 — N_WIDE_MERGE: no action unless it is slow (`check --cost` measures it). query feed: read cost grows — snapback4 why N_WIDE_MERGE",
            "by design — what a declaration implies; no action needed for online use:",
            "note: schema.q:71:1 — N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED: no action for an online app. 2 synced maintains — snapback4 why N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED",
            "note: schema.q:78:10 — N_SYNC_ORDER: no action for an online app. query a reads by .id; the client needs the server — snapback4 why N_SYNC_ORDER",
            "note: N_SYNC_ORDER (as above) also for query b (schema.q:87:10) — snapback4 why N_SYNC_ORDER",
        ]);
        assert!(render(Vec::new()).is_empty());
    }
    #[test]
    fn insert_and_result_privacy_are_review_actions_not_performance_advice(){
        for code in ["N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT","N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE"]{
            assert_eq!(tier(code),Tier::Review);
            assert!(summary_order("N_ROLE_WIDE_READ")<summary_order(code));
            assert!(verdict(code,"",false).starts_with("review:"));
        }
    }

}

#[cfg(test)]
mod integration20_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn source_authority_advice_never_says_to_ignore_it_until_slow() {
        for code in ["N_LOOKUP_FILTERED", "N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN"] {
            assert_eq!(tier(code), Tier::Review);
            assert!(verdict(code, "", false).starts_with("review:"));
        }
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#165-review-clarification-exact-detector-and-command-boundaries — repeated sites must not consume independent advice classes.
pub(crate) fn shortlist(notes:&mut Vec<Note>){
 let mut ordinary=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
 notes.retain(|note|{
  if snapback4_lang::security::note_priority(&note.code)<=3 || (tier(&note.code)==Tier::Review && !hygiene(&note.code)){return true;}
  if ordinary.contains(&note.code){return true;}
  if ordinary.len()<5{ordinary.insert(note.code.clone());true}else{false}
 });
}
#[cfg(test)]mod shortlist_tests{use super::*;
#[test]fn repeated_classes_keep_distinct_audiences_without_hiding_security(){
 let mut notes=vec![];
 for code in ["N_ROLE_WIDE_READ","N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER","N_NEVER_COMPLETE","N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED","N_NO_HORIZON","N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT","N_SECURITY_INTENT"]{for n in 0..8{notes.push(Note{site:format!("schema.q:{n}:1"),code:code.into(),summary:String::new(),form:None,compact:None});}}
 shortlist(&mut notes);assert_eq!(notes.len(),56);assert!(notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_SECURITY_INTENT"));assert_eq!(notes.iter().filter(|n|n.code=="N_NEVER_COMPLETE").count(),8);
}}

fn hygiene(code:&str)->bool{matches!(code,"N_LOOKUP_FILTERED"|"N_QUERY_FILTERED"|"N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN"|"N_SPARSE_FILTER"|"N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE")}
#[cfg(test)]mod authority_visibility_tests{
 use super::*;
 #[test]fn no_authorization_review_is_cut_while_lookup_hygiene_is_visible(){
   let codes=snapback4_lang::guide::codes();
   let mut notes=codes.iter().map(|code|Note{site:"schema.q:1:1".into(),code:(*code).into(),summary:String::new(),form:None,compact:None}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   notes.sort_by_key(|n|summary_order(&n.code));shortlist(&mut notes);
   for code in codes.into_iter().filter(|code|tier(code)==Tier::Review&&!hygiene(code)){
     assert!(notes.iter().any(|n|n.code==code),"authorization/correctness review {code} was cut");
   }
   assert!(summary_order("N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT")<summary_order("N_LOOKUP_FILTERED"));
 }
}

#[cfg(test)] mod gated_note_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test] fn every_security_gate_is_rendered_as_review_never_performance() {
        for code in snapback4_lang::security::GATED_NOTE_CODES {
            assert!(snapback4_lang::security::gated_note(code));
            assert_eq!(tier(code), Tier::Review, "{code}");
            assert_eq!(summary_order(code).0, Tier::Review, "{code}");
            assert!(!verdict(code, "", false).contains("slow"), "{code}");
            let text=render(vec![Note{site:"schema.q:1:1".into(),code:(*code).into(),summary:"individual decision required".into(),form:None,compact:None}]).join("\n");
            assert!(!text.contains("performance"), "{code}: {text}");
        }
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — the native CLI embeds this same command table; helper help needs no binary.
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
const commands = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL('./cli-commands.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8'));
export function synopsis(verb) {
  const command = commands.find(command => command.verb === verb);
  if (!command) throw Error(`unknown command ${verb}`);
  return `snapback4 ${verb}${command.positional ? ` ${command.positional}` : ''}`
    + command.flags.filter(flag => !flag.retired).map(flag => ` [--${flag.name}${flag.value ? ` ${flag.value}` : ''}]`).join('');
}
