//! Validate the complete command before any project, store or host work.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — admission, every synopsis and flag help share this command table.
use std::sync::OnceLock;
#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
struct Flag { name: String, value: String, #[serde(default)] retired: bool }
#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
struct Command { verb: String, positional: String, flags: Vec<Flag>, #[serde(skip)] allowed: String }
fn commands() -> &'static [Command] {
    static TABLE: OnceLock<Vec<Command>> = OnceLock::new();
    TABLE.get_or_init(|| {
        let mut commands: Vec<Command> = serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../../packages/snapback4/bin/cli-commands.json")).expect("embedded command table");
        for command in &mut commands { command.allowed = command.flags.iter().map(|flag|flag.name.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" "); }
        commands
    })
}
fn command(verb: &str) -> Option<&'static Command> { commands().iter().find(|command|command.verb==verb) }
pub(crate) fn allowed(verb: &str) -> &'static str { command(verb).map_or("", |command| command.allowed.as_str()) }

pub fn validate(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), String> {
    let verb = args.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("");
    // Pipe-owned private commands have positional protocols, not public flags.
    if matches!(verb, "--internal-witness-process" | "--internal-worker-watchdog") { return Ok(()); }
    let mut iter = args.iter().skip(1).peekable();
    while let Some(arg) = iter.next() {
        if arg == "--" { break; }
        if arg == "-h" { continue; }
        let Some(flag) = arg.strip_prefix("--") else {
            if arg.starts_with('-') && !arg[1..].starts_with(|c: char| c.is_ascii_digit()) {
                return Err(if verb == "witness" { "E_INPUT: witness has an invalid flag; see guide witness".into() } else { format!("E_INPUT: {verb} takes no {arg}") });
            }
            continue;
        };
        let (name, inline) = flag.split_once('=').map_or((flag, None), |(name, value)| (name, Some(value)));
        if name != "help" && !allowed(verb).split_whitespace().any(|known| known == name) {
            if verb == "rollback" && name == "dry-run" { return Err("E_INPUT: rollback preview is --check; use rollback <dir> [--to N|latest] --check".into()); }
            // Witness arguments may contain bearer material even in a flag name.
            return Err(if verb == "witness" { "E_INPUT: witness has an unknown flag; see guide witness".into() } else { format!("E_INPUT: {verb} takes no --{name}") });
        }
        if switch(name) {
            if inline.is_some() { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} takes no value")); }
        } else if inline.is_none() {
            // Retired spellings route to their existing no-work explanation,
            // even with a missing value; new unknown flags still refuse here.
            if retired(verb,name) {
                if iter.peek().is_some_and(|value| !value.starts_with("--")) { iter.next(); }
                continue;
            }
            let Some(value) = iter.next() else { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} needs a value")); };
            // Search text is literal, including leading dashes and punctuation.
            if value.starts_with("--") && !matches!(name, "find" | "section") {
                return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} needs a value"));
            }
        }
    }
    let limit=match verb {"deploy"|"rollback"|"prune"|"stop"=>Some(1),"releases"=>Some(2),_=>None};
    if limit.is_some_and(|limit|crate::verbs::parse(args).positionals.len()>limit) {
        return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} has extra positional arguments; see `{verb} --help`"));
    }
    let has = |name: &str| args.iter().skip(1).any(|arg| arg == &format!("--{name}") || arg.starts_with(&format!("--{name}=")));
    if verb == "check" {
        if has("ack-security")!=has("reason"){return Err("E_INPUT: --ack-security ID requires --reason TEXT".into());}
        if has("ack-security")&&["ack-notes","fix","store","release","emit-lean","audit"].iter().any(|f|has(f)){return Err("E_INPUT: individual security review cannot combine with --ack-notes, --fix, saved-store/release, --emit-lean or --audit".into());}
        if has("release") && !has("store") { return Err("E_INPUT: check --release requires --store <path>".into()); }
        if has("as") && !has("residency") { return Err("E_INPUT: check --as requires --residency".into()); }
        let incompatible = if has("emit-lean") { Some(("--emit-lean", &["cost","residency","fix","verbose","store","fixture","as","release","ack-notes","audit"][..])) }
            else if has("release") { Some(("--store --release", &["cost","residency","fix","fixture","as","ack-notes"][..])) } else { None };
        if let Some((mode,flags)) = incompatible {
            if let Some(flag) = flags.iter().find(|flag|has(flag)) { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: check {mode} does not accept --{flag}")); }
        }
    }
    if verb == "serve" && has("wait-stopped") && args.iter().any(|arg|arg.starts_with("--") && !matches!(arg.as_str(),"--wait-stopped"|"--help")) {
        return Err("E_INPUT: serve --wait-stopped takes only a deployment directory; it does not start or signal a server".into());
    }
    if verb == "rollback" && has("bridge") && !has("check") { return Err("E_INPUT: rollback --bridge requires --check; it writes a bridge starter without activating it".into()); }
    if verb == "check" && has("audit") && ["cost","residency","fix","store","fixture","as","release","emit-lean","ack-notes"].iter().any(|f|has(f)) { return Err("E_INPUT: check --audit takes only --json or --verbose".into()); }
    if verb == "test" && has("verbose") && !has("residency") { return Err("E_INPUT: test --verbose requires --residency".into()); }
    if verb == "run" && has("token-env") && !has("release") { return Err("E_INPUT: run --token-env requires --release".into()); }
    if verb == "why" && has("instance") && crate::verbs::parse(args).positionals.first().map(String::as_str) != Some("recent") { return Err("E_INPUT: why --instance requires why recent".into()); }
    if verb == "token" && has("port") && has("release") { return Err("E_INPUT: token --port selects dev and cannot combine with --release".into()); }
    if verb == "token" && has("quiet") && has("json") { return Err("E_INPUT: token takes either --quiet or --json".into()); }
    Ok(())
}

fn retired(verb: &str, name: &str) -> bool {
    command(verb).is_some_and(|command|command.flags.iter().any(|flag|flag.name==name && flag.retired))
}
pub fn switch(name: &str) -> bool { name=="help" || commands().iter().flat_map(|command|&command.flags).any(|flag|flag.name==name && flag.value.is_empty()) }

#[cfg(test)]
mod integration_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn wave9_web_flags_and_upgrade_keep_shared_validation() {
        let args = ["witness", "web", "dist", "--url", "http://127.0.0.1", "--as", "alice", "--email", "alice@example.test", "--password-env", "PRIVATE_PASSWORD", "--mutation", "add", "--args", "{}", "--timeout", "30", "--export", "dist"].map(String::from);
        assert!(validate(&args).is_ok());
        for verb in ["upgrade", "witness"] {
            assert!(validate(&[verb.into(), "--unknown-wave9".into()]).is_err());
        }
        assert!(validate(&["upgrade".into(), "0.4.25".into()]).is_ok());
        assert!(validate(&["upgrade".into(), "--to".into(), "0.4.25".into(), "--json".into()]).is_ok());
        assert!(validate(&["witness".into(), "web".into(), "--password-env".into()]).is_err());
    }
}

fn value(verb: &str, name: &str) -> String {
    command(verb).and_then(|command|command.flags.iter().find(|flag|flag.name==name)).filter(|flag|!flag.value.is_empty()).map(|flag|format!(" {}",flag.value)).unwrap_or_default()
}

/// Generated from the same flag table used before dispatch and side effects.
pub(crate) fn help(verb: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::from("Flags (validated before any project or store work):\n");
    for name in allowed(verb).split_whitespace().chain(["help"]) {
        let value = value(verb, name);
        let description = match (verb, name) {
            ("deploy", "schema") => " — deploy a saved complete source; keep it outside snapback/",
            ("deploy", "staging") => " — classify a new disposable deployment as staging; existing production stores refuse",
            ("dev", "unsafe-dev-doors") => " — explicitly open unauthenticated local fixture/clock/persona doors for password-only apps",
            ("test", "source") => " — test a saved complete source; keep it outside snapback/",
            ("doctor", "repair-expiry-orphan") => " — offline recovery of one physically missing expiring parent, table:id; stop its host and back up first (guide expire)",
            ("clock", "no-drain") => " — pause background maintenance for a mutation deadline test; clock drain resumes it",
            ("clock", "allow-held") => " — explicitly accept already processed future schedule boundaries",
            ("clock", "expire-sessions") => " — age staging sessions with business time (default: preserve remaining lifetime)",
            ("clock", "each-boundary") => " — fire every crossed schedule window in chronological order (default: latest window only)",
            (_, "help") => " — print this verb's help (-h also works)",
            _ if retired(verb,name) => " — retired spelling; the command explains its replacement before work",
            ("doctor", "instance") => " — inspect one dev instance's health and refusals",
            ("doctor", "url") => " — inspect the backend at this URL",
            ("doctor", "latency") => " — measure request and stream latency",
            ("doctor", "reset") => " — reset latency observations before measuring",
            ("witness", "password-env") => " — environment variable holding the sign-in password",
            ("witness", "background") => " — run the native witness in the background; inspect witness status",
            ("witness", "if-present") => " — skip an absent native witness installation",
            ("witness", "environment-skip") => " — report an unavailable native environment as skipped",
            ("witness", "initialize") => " — initialize the witness installation",
            ("witness", "wait") => " — wait for the background witness to finish",
            (_, "json") => " — print machine-readable JSON on stdout",
            ("upgrade", "quiet") => " — print only per-project actions; suppress successful install/check/history output",
            (_, "fixture-only") => " — allow zero business cases for fixture loading and query probes",
            (_, "bridge") => " — with --check, write current source and this app's rollback checklist into a new directory",
            (_, "save-version") => " — save deployed sources, contemporary fixtures and immutable release label in a new directory",
            (_, "quiet") => " — print only the bearer token, with no successful stderr output",
            (_, "list") => " — list available guide cards",
            (_, "full") => " — print the complete guide card",
            (_, "sections") => " — print the card's numbered section index",
            (_, "section") => " — select a heading, topic or section number",
            (_, "find") => " — search every word (a|b or a OR b matches either)",
            (_, "as") => " — select a persona or principal for the operation/proof",
            ("dev" | "serve", "port") => " — backend port (0 chooses a free port; A-B tries an inclusive range)",
            ("init" | "new", "port") => " — backend port, 1–65535 (frontend defaults to the next port)",
            (_, "port") => " — running backend port, 1–65535",
            (_, "port-range") => " — inclusive range for backend and frontend ports",
            (_, "web-port") => " — frontend port, overriding the next-port default",
            (_, "bind") => " — listening IP address (default 127.0.0.1)",
            (_, "i-understand-cleartext") => " — explicitly allow a non-loopback HTTP listener",
            (_, "worker") => " — production worker entry path",
            (_, "long-waits") => " — held viewer request capacity (default 1024, max 4096)",
            (_, "long-waits-per-principal") => " — held viewer requests per principal (default 16)",
            (_, "claim-waits") => " — held worker claim capacity (default 32, max 1024)",
            (_, "clock-offset") => " — start dev or a classified staging clock at an offset, for example +25h",
            (_, "resume-clock") => " — resume the persisted development store's clock",
            (_, "now") => " — pin a local run's clock (milliseconds or +duration)",
            (_, "clock") => " — reset the development clock with --clock reset",
            (_, "seed") => " — reseed from a fixture directory or rows.json in the reset transaction",
            (_, "release") => " — release directory (check --store instead selects N or latest)",
            ("sql", "dormant") => " — inspect retained dormant rows and cells; requires --release",
                if super::retired(verb,name) { assert!(page.lines().find(|line|line.trim_start().starts_with(&format!("--{name} "))).unwrap().contains("retired spelling")); }
            }
        }
    }
}

/// One synopsis for top-level help, per-verb help and usage errors. Flags come
/// directly from the admission table, including value arity and retired forms.
pub(crate) const VERBS: &[&str] = &["init","new","audit","check","device","types","dev","deploy","rollback","prune","releases","serve","serve-check","stop","run","clock","seed","reset","test","why","guide","data","sql","token","doctor","upgrade","witness","web-shell"];
pub(crate) fn synopsis(verb: &str) -> String {
    let positional = command(verb).map(|command|command.positional.as_str()).filter(|position|!position.is_empty()).map(|position|format!(" {position}")).unwrap_or_default();
    let mut out = format!("snapback4 {verb}{positional}");
    for name in allowed(verb).split_whitespace().filter(|name| !retired(verb,name)) {
        let value = value(verb, name);
        out.push_str(&format!(" [--{name}{value}]"));
    }
    out
}
pub(crate) fn top_help() -> String {
    let mut out = String::from("snapback4 — the scale-to-zero backend with a partition on every device\n\n");
    for verb in VERBS { out.push_str(&format!("  {}\n      {}\n", synopsis(verb), match *verb {
        "init"=>"Create a schema or a web/Expo starter; init --adapt fits an existing starter.",
        "new"=>"Create a full reference app: --template social, or chat with --web/--expo.",
        "audit"=>"Report security intent, conditional exposure paths and grant writers.",
        "check"=>"Compile the schema, check store adoption, write client types and report readiness.",
        "device"=>"Run the native device JSON protocol over stdin/stdout.", "types"=>"Compile and write generated client types.",
        "dev"=>"Serve a development backend; print its URL on stdout, readiness and refusals on stderr.",
        "deploy"=>"Capture and activate an immutable release; running serve adopts it.", "rollback"=>"Preview or activate an existing release; preserve dormant storage.",
        "prune"=>"Preview or explicitly remove dormant release storage.", "releases"=>"Inspect database-authoritative release history and storage.",
        "serve"=>"Serve the active production release; bearer sessions only, behind a TLS proxy.",
        "serve-check"=>"Check an npm serve script through the packaged Node CLI; keep credential stdout private.",
        "stop"=>"Gracefully stop the retained server that owns a deployment directory.",
        "run"=>"Run one operation or system job locally or against a serving backend.",
        "clock"=>"Advance the development clock, sweep expiry and run due jobs.", "reset"=>"Empty a development backend and optionally reseed it atomically.",
        "test"=>"Load fixtures, run cases and sweep queries as their personas; --residency: acquisition report only; add --verbose to run queries and print meters.", "why"=>"Explain a diagnostic, operation, table, work id or recent refusal.",
        "guide"=>"List cards or read a card/alias; --sections indexes it, --section selects one.",
        "data"=>"Inspect stored rows as an administrator; bypasses read rules.", "sql"=>"Read-only administrative SQL inspection; bypasses read rules.",
        "token"=>"Mint a service seat's bearer; --quiet is token-only, --json returns metadata.",
        "doctor"=>"Inspect backend health or latency; --json returns a machine-readable report.",
        "upgrade"=>"Upgrade core and installed adapters together; --to permits an explicit downgrade.",
        "witness"=>"Prove browser writes, device acquisition or installed iOS behavior.", "web-shell"=>"Install an offline shell for a static web export (npm wrapper).", _=>"",
    })); }
    out.push_str("  snapback4 --version\n\nsnapback4 <verb> --help explains each flag, output and exit codes.");
    out
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod surface_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn every_help_and_usage_projects_the_same_synopsis() {
        let top = top_help();
        assert_eq!(commands().iter().map(|command|command.verb.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), VERBS);
        for verb in VERBS {
            let synopsis = synopsis(verb);
            assert!(top.lines().any(|line| line.trim() == synopsis), "{verb}");
            assert_eq!(crate::verb_help::page(verb).unwrap().lines().next(), Some(synopsis.as_str()), "{verb}");
            let actual = synopsis.split("[--").skip(1).map(|part|part.split([' ',']']).next().unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            let expected = allowed(verb).split_whitespace().filter(|flag| !retired(verb,flag)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            assert_eq!(actual, expected, "{verb}");
        }
        assert!(synopsis("deploy").contains("[--schema file.q]"));
        assert!(synopsis("init").contains("[--port-range A-B]"));
        assert!(synopsis("upgrade").contains("[--to version]"));
    }
    #[test]

            assert!(validate(&args.iter().map(|s|s.to_string()).collect::<Vec<_>>()).unwrap_err().starts_with("E_INPUT:"), "{args:?}");
        }
        assert!(validate(&["why","--instance","x","recent"].map(String::from)).is_ok());

# LLP 3000.036.004: Security invariants and exposure

**Type:** RFC
**Status:** Accepted (SEC design revisions 3/5 and SEC2 amendment/implementation round 5: Muse and fresh Codex READY; qualification in progress)
**Systems:** Snapback 4 Quarry authorization, check, audit, generated security tests, validation
**Author:** Codex for Charlie Cheever (SEC)
**Date:** 2026-10-18
**Revised:** 2026-10-24 (DX9 wave-26 amendments; Muse and fresh Codex READY); 2026-10-24 (Integration 24 §15.1 boundary clarification, governed by LLP 3000.036.004.000 §9); 2026-10-23 (SEC7 §16 design: Muse and fresh Codex READY; implementation qualification in progress); previously 2026-10-21 (SEC5 §14 and SEC6 §15; both independently reviewed; SEC6 scoped qualification complete; code snapshot4578e98bed)
**Related:** [LLP 3000.036](./3000.036-what-the-app-farm-asks-of-the-rule-language.rfc.md), [LLP 3000.035](./3000.035-quarry-semantics-in-lean.plan.md), [LLP 3000.014](./3000.014-a-child-reads-through-its-parent.rfc.md), [LLP 3000.038](./3000.038-file-assets.rfc.md)

## 18. Wave-26 security decisions (DX9)

Implementer: Codex for Charlie Cheever, 2026-10-24. Revision 3: Muse and fresh Codex READY; implementation is in the DX9 commits. Qualification and limitations: WORKSTREAM-REPORT.md at the worktree root.
This supersedes §17.3's prohibition on family disposition only for the
advisory audience/restatement family. Gates remain individual specification
decisions, including every new SEC10 gate; one gate registry decides ranking.
The default check summary names the number of pending decisions and the next
command, even when callers read only ^check:. Each review-tier item appears
once as a question with one-line alternatives: repair enforcement, record an
intentional specification decision, or add independent intent/test evidence.
Performance/design information remains separate. JSON retains every original
diagnostic, site, ID, source binding and acknowledgment. Gate explanation and
disposition command share one decision entry, never a second note listing.

check --ack-security audience:<family-id> --reason '<spec rationale>' records
one rationale for the exact displayed set of current non-gated audience notes.
The eligible codes are exactly N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE, N_ROLE_WIDE_READ,
N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE and N_BROAD_READ, excluding any code in GATED_NOTE_CODES.
The family ID hashes analyzer revision sec9-3, their sorted individual IDs and
semantic source bindings;
each member is still written as an individual existing version-1 record. A
new member, changed dependency, unknown/stale family, blank reason, compilation
failure or reachable refutation cannot be cleared. No gate, derived contract,
non-audience heuristic, or authored contradiction enters this family. Existing
writer lock and source race checks bind the complete set. All member entries
are added in memory and published in one temp+rename of security-reviews.json;
there is no partial-family write. A crash before rename leaves the prior file.
The individual record analyzer revision also becomes sec9-3.
Output names the member subjects and count so the command is reviewable.

The static rule analyzer's Verdict.direct_rule=Proved alongside
Verdict.status=Unproved is rendered as
"enforced by rule; additional paths not proved", with exact blockers in verbose
and JSON (Verdict.reasons, Verdict.obligations and their paths/status/repair).
Whole-invariant Proved/Refuted/Unproved semantics never change, and
no finite campaign promotes a proof. A check line counts asset-bearing columns (ColumnType.contains_asset),
Table.column_reads entries, and N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION diagnostics. Asset owner
custody is the runtime asset ledger/placement contract, not a linter theorem.
Role diagnostics use the existing lexical authority walker (helper depth 32)
and existing bounded privilege analyzer; the line says "detected (bounded
analysis)" and does not characterize unreachable/unsupported paths as safe. Never print an unconditional zero-role-hole claim.

The old N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE detector is folded into the single
N_SWEEP_DEADLINE path detector (its existing why explanation remains readable).
It correlates a scheduled status assignment and a mutation
guard by the same table, column and literal state/polarity. Sharing a status
column is insufficient. Mutations admitting the state the sweep removes need
the deadline question; mutations requiring a disjoint state are quiet. Unknown
state relationships stay explicit conservative questions. For each actual write, the lexical authority walker supplies its prefix require
statements and the chosen branch condition or its negation. Finite bool/enum
states are evaluated under those guards with unknown treated as possibly
admitted. Empty intersection of the job's and mutation's admitted pre-states
suppresses the question; otherwise it is conservative. Only recognized guards on the same table/deadline and resolved read-key identity that exclude the
sweep's due-time window suppress it. Normalize direct field-versus-now
comparisons into before/equal/after masks; transpose reversed comparisons,
complement not, intersect and, and union or only when both arms are known.
Unknown/affine-offset relationships retain the question. A <= sweep is excluded
by >, not >= (which overlaps equality) or <=. PeriodEnd in the job denotes its
elapsed boundary; mutation guards compare against now. Tests cover polarity,
equality overlap, negation and sibling branches.
Alias names are resolved to the underlying read/table/key; string alias-name
equality alone is neither required nor sufficient for suppression. Job clocks
cannot suppress mutation advice. A server-now guard must dominate the particular guarded write path, not occur in an unrelated arm.
Tests include unrelated deadlines on shared status columns and actual races.

    /// Activate using the owning host's clock for parent liveness and captures.
    pub fn new_at(store: impl Into<AnyStore>, mut schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>, now: i64) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        let mut store = store.into();
        let programs = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();

        columns::validate_before_prepare(&schema)?;
        activation_facts::prepare(&mut schema)?;
        schema.check_row_view_audiences()?;
        schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
        schema.prepare_delivery_lookup();
        let issues = crate::schema::validate(&schema);
        if let Some(issue) = issues.first() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{}: {}", issue.path, issue.message)));
        }
        let (generation, metadata) = Self::activate(&mut store, &schema, &programs, None, now, true, None, None)?.into_inner();

    }

    pub fn open_at(store: impl Into<AnyStore>, now: i64) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::open_selected_at(store.into(), now, None)
    }

    fn open_selected_at(mut store: AnyStore, now: i64, expected_release: Option<u64>) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = store.opening_transaction()?;
        tx.check_selected_release(expected_release)?;
        let crate::activation::Backend(generation, schema, programs) = tx.opening_backend()?;
        let kept = crate::activation::Backend(generation, schema.clone(), programs.clone());
        let generation = u64::try_from(generation).map_err(|_| RuntimeError::Store("negative stored generation".into()))?;
        let schema = serde_json::from_str(&schema).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e, |e| RuntimeError::Store(format!("invalid stored schema: {e}"))))?;
        crate::schema::timeline::check_version(&schema)?;
        timeline::check_no_rebuilds(&tx, &schema)?;
        schema.check_row_view_audiences()?;
        let programs = serde_json::from_str(&programs).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(format!("invalid stored programs: {e}")))?;
        tx.admit(&schema, &programs)?;
        let mut schema: Schema = schema;
        let stored_schema_hash = schema.hash();
        schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
        schema.prepare_delivery_lookup();
        let schema_hash = schema.hash();
        // Upgrade derived candidates from older binaries using the stored
        // backend, never a caller's source. The repair logs its row changes.
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9.3 — expiry index adoption is required even without physical cap counters.
        let (generation, metadata) = if stored_schema_hash == schema_hash
            && tx.opening_marker(crate::metadata::SchemaMarker::LiveSumCandidates)?.as_deref() == Some("1")
            && (!schema.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none())
                || tx.opening_marker(crate::metadata::SchemaMarker::MaintainedIds)?.as_deref() == Some(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION))
            && (!crate::rows::caps::required(&schema) || tx.opening_marker(crate::metadata::SchemaMarker::CapCounts)?.as_deref() == Some(&schema_hash))
            && (!schema.tables.values().any(|table| table.not_before.is_some())
                || tx.opening_marker(crate::metadata::SchemaMarker::TimePublications)?.as_deref() == Some("1")) { (generation, AnyStore::finish_open(tx)?) }
            else { tx.rollback()?; Self::activate(&mut store, &schema, &programs, None, now, true, expected_release, Some(&kept))?.into_inner() };
        let dev_effects = metadata.dev_effects;
        store.accept_open(); store.exclude_ephemeral(&schema);
        Ok(Self { #[cfg(feature="postgres")] revision_cache: Default::default(), #[cfg(feature="postgres")] code_native_digest: metadata.native_digest, #[cfg(feature = "evaluation-support")] evaluation_precharge: Metered::default(), timeline_schedule: timeline::Schedule::default(), ephemeral: std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(metadata.store_id.clone(), generation, &schema)), store_id: metadata.store_id, store, schema, programs, generation, expiry_after: BTreeMap::new(), dev_effects, ceilings: Ceilings::default(), entropy: crate::ids::seed(), minter: crate::ids::Mint::default(), #[cfg(feature = "native")] native: None, #[cfg(feature = "native")] native_logs: native::Logs::default(), presence: None })
    }

//! Connection custody for the neutral server handle. No driver borrow escapes a
//! shared-receiver composite; conflicting borrows are typed store errors.
// @ref LLP 3000.009.001.000#3.4 — scoped family access and a same-store reader factory.
use super::store::{Store, Tx};
use crate::{RuntimeError, store_port::report::AdoptionReport};
use std::cell::{RefCell, RefMut};

pub struct Handle {
    pub(crate) store: RefCell<Store>,
    pub(crate) report: AdoptionReport,
    pub(super) connection: (postgres::Config, String),
    pub(super) fresh: bool,
    pub(super) readers: RefCell<Option<std::sync::Arc<super::pipeline::Pool>>>,
    pub(super) bootstrap_id: Option<String>,
}
impl Handle {
    pub(crate) fn borrow(&self) -> Result<RefMut<'_, Store>, RuntimeError> {
        self.store.try_borrow_mut().map_err(|_| RuntimeError::Store("Postgres store already borrowed".into()))
    }
    pub(crate) fn scoped<T>(&self, run: impl FnOnce(RefCell<&mut Store>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
        run(RefCell::new(&mut *self.borrow()?))
    }
    pub(crate) fn read<T>(&self, run: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
        let mut store = self.borrow()?;
        let tx = store.transaction(None)?;
        let result = run(&tx)?;
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(result)
    }
    pub(crate) fn reader(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        if self.fresh { return Err(RuntimeError::StoreUnsupported { family: "opening" }); }
        let before = self.read(|tx| Ok((crate::metadata::MetadataReader::store_identity(tx)?, crate::activation::ActivationReader::opening_backend(tx)?)))?;
        let existing = self.readers.borrow().clone();
        let pool = if let Some(pool) = existing { pool } else {
            let pool = super::pipeline::Pool::new(&self.connection.0, &self.connection.1, self.borrow()?.fence.as_ref().map(|f| f.tag()).transpose()?)?;
            *self.readers.borrow_mut() = Some(pool.clone()); pool
        };
        let reader = Self::connect_reader(self.connection.0.clone(), &self.connection.1, self.borrow()?.fence.clone(), pool)?;
        reader.store.borrow_mut().read_grant=self.borrow()?.read_grant.clone();
        let after = reader.read(|tx| Ok((crate::metadata::MetadataReader::store_identity(tx)?, crate::activation::ActivationReader::opening_backend(tx)?)))?;
        if before != after { return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("reader store identity or generation changed; reopen".into())); }
        Ok(reader)
    }
    pub(crate) fn write<T>(&self, run: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
        let mut store = self.borrow()?;
        let tx = store.transaction(Some(super::WriteMode::Serialized))?;
        let result = run(&tx)?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(result)
    }
}

/// Logical handles have separate statement/answer caches. Only transport and
/// connection custody are shared. No authority or result survives a request.
pub(crate) enum Connection {
    Sync(postgres::Client),
    Reader {
        slot: Arc<Slot>,
        _pool: Arc<Pool>,
        session: bool,
        lease: Option<OwnedRwLockWriteGuard<()>>,
    },
}
impl std::ops::Deref for Connection {
    type Target = postgres::Client;
    fn deref(&self) -> &Self::Target {
        match self {
            Self::Sync(c) => c,
            _ => panic!("synchronous-only Postgres operation on a reader"),
        }
    }
}
impl std::ops::DerefMut for Connection {
    fn deref_mut(&mut self) -> &mut Self::Target {
        match self {
            Self::Sync(c) => c,
            _ => panic!("synchronous-only Postgres operation on a reader"),
        }
    }
}
impl Connection {
    pub fn reader(pool: Arc<Pool>) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Ok(Self::Reader {
            slot: pool.slot()?,
            _pool: pool,
            session: false,
            lease: None,
        })
    }
    pub fn into_sync(self) -> postgres::Client {
        match self {
            Self::Sync(c) => c,
            _ => panic!("writer expected"),
        }
    }
    pub(super) fn prepared_cleanup(&self, name: &str) -> Option<PreparedName> {
        match self {
            Self::Reader { slot, .. } => Some(PreparedName {
                slot: slot.clone(),
                name: name.into(),
            }),
            Self::Sync(_) => None,
        }
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(super) fn is_closed(&self) -> bool {
        match self {
            Self::Sync(c) => c.is_closed(),
            Self::Reader { slot, .. } => slot.client.is_closed(),
        }
    }
    // Only called before a new transaction/request. An in-flight query is
    // never replayed; its original future still receives the transport error.
    pub(super) fn refresh_closed(&mut self) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        let pool = match self {
            Self::Reader { slot, _pool, .. } if slot.client.is_closed() => _pool.clone(),
            _ => return Ok(false),
        };

export interface StagingAccount {
  kind: "user"; role: string; email: string; password: string; principal: string; token: string; expiresAt: number;
}
export type StagingSessions = Record<string, StagingAccount[]>;
export type ProvisionedAccount = StagingAccount;
export type ProvisionedSessions = StagingSessions;
export interface ProvisionAccountsOptions extends SeedAccountsOptions {

export interface SeedAccountsOptions {
  roles: Record<string, number>;
  provision: { readonly name: string };
  /** Service seat authorized by the provisioning mutation. Default operator. */
  service?: string;
  args?: (account: StagingAccount) => Record<string, unknown>;
  /** Whole setup deadline, including rate-limit waits. Default 5 minutes. */
  timeoutMs?: number;
}

function validatePlan(options: SeedAccountsOptions) {
  // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#8-wave-18-developer-loop-amendment-dx4 — custom argument keys override defaults; the seat grants no extra authority.
  const service = options.service ?? "operator";
  const roles = Object.entries(options.roles), timeout = options.timeoutMs ?? 300_000;
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(service) || !Number.isSafeInteger(timeout) || timeout < 1 || !roles.length || roles.some(([role, n]) => !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}$/.test(role) || !Number.isSafeInteger(n) || n < 1)
      || roles.reduce((sum, [, n]) => sum + n, 0) > 100 || !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*(?:\.[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*)*$/.test(options.provision?.name ?? "")) {
    throw Error("E_STAGING_INPUT: provide 1..100 accounts across named roles, a mutation handle and a positive timeoutMs");
  }
  return {service, roles, timeout};
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — real credentials and ordinary operator mutations, never system seeding or auth bypass.
/** @throws E_STAGING_INPUT for invalid plans; E_STAGING for production/dev hosts; E_STAGING_TIMEOUT, E_STAGING_SIGNUP or E_STAGING_PROVISION for incomplete setup. Partial accounts/grants may exist; inspect or discard the staging store before retrying. */
async function accounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: SeedAccountsOptions, environment: "staging" | "production", roster?: Roster, save: () => void = () => {}): Promise<StagingSessions> {
  const {service, roles, timeout} = validatePlan(options);
  const end = performance.now() + timeout;
  const partial = environment === "staging" ? "inspect or discard this staging deployment" : "inspect partial production accounts/grants before retrying";
  const remaining = () => {
    const ms = Math.ceil(end - performance.now());
    if (ms <= 0) throw Error(`E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: setup incomplete; partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
    return ms;
  };
  const request = (path: string, init?: RequestInit) => fetch(host.url + path, { ...init, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(Math.min(remaining(), 30_000)) });
  const config = await request("/auth/config");
  const identity = await config.json();
  if (!config.ok || identity.environment !== environment || identity.development !== false || !identity.password) {
    throw Error(environment === "staging"
      ? "E_STAGING: account seeding requires serve on a fresh deploy --staging with password identity; production and dev refuse before creating users"
      : "E_PRODUCTION_ACCOUNTS: account provisioning requires serve on a production deploy with password identity; staging and dev refuse before creating users");
  }
  const operator = await host.operatorToken(service);
  if (environment === "production") {
    const response = await request("/schema", { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${operator}` } }), schema = await response.json();
    const named = response.ok && Array.isArray(schema.service_identities) && schema.service_identities.includes(`service:${service}`);
    if (!named) process.stderr.write(`snapback4: WARNING minted service:${service}, but the served schema reports no explicit positive authorization mention for that service identity; rules still decide every operation and provisionAccounts(${options.provision.name}) will likely refuse.\n`);
  }
  const sessions: StagingSessions = Object.create(null);
  for (const [role, count] of roles) {
    sessions[role] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      let fresh = false;
      let entry = roster?.entries.find(entry => entry.role === role && entry.index === i);
      if (!entry) {
        fresh = true;
        entry = { role, index: i, email: `${environment}-${role}-${randomUUID()}@example.test`, password: randomBytes(24).toString("base64url"), id: randomUUID(), done: false };
        roster?.entries.push(entry); save(); // Persist identity before the first possibly ambiguous write.
      }
      const { email, password } = entry;
      let reply: Record<string, unknown>;
      if (roster && !fresh) {
        const result = await identityRequest(host.url + "/auth/login", {
          method:"POST", headers:{"content-type":"application/json"}, body:JSON.stringify({email,password}),
        }, fetch, {timeoutMs:remaining()}).catch(error => {
          if ((error as {code?:string})?.code === "E_IDENTITY_TIMEOUT") throw Error(`E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: login deadline expired; ${partial}`);
          remaining(); throw error;
        });
        reply = result.json;
        if (!reply.session && (entry.principal || (reply.denied as {code?:string})?.code !== "E_AUTH")) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: saved account login failed; inspect roster and deployment; no replacement account created");
      } else reply = {};
      if (!reply.session) {
      const result = await identityRequest(host.url + "/auth/signup", {
        method:"POST", headers:{"content-type":"application/json"}, body:JSON.stringify({email,password}),
      }, fetch, {timeoutMs:remaining()}).catch(error => {
        if ((error as {code?: string})?.code === "E_IDENTITY_TIMEOUT") throw Error(`E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: signup deadline expired; partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
        remaining(); throw error;
      });
      reply = result.json;
      }
      if ((reply.denied as {code?:string} | undefined)?.code === "E_RATE_LIMIT") {
        throw Error("E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: identity retry budget exhausted; increase timeoutMs or inspect partial accounts/grants before retrying");
      }
      const session = reply.session as {principal?:string;kind?:string;token?:string;expiresAt?:number} | undefined;
      if (typeof session?.principal !== "string" || typeof session.token !== "string" || typeof session.expiresAt !== "number" || session.kind !== "user") {
        throw Error(`E_STAGING_SIGNUP: signup refused (${(reply.denied as {code?:string} | undefined)?.code ?? "invalid reply"}); partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
      }
      const account: StagingAccount = { kind: session.kind, role, email, password, principal: session.principal, token: session.token, expiresAt: session.expiresAt };
      if (entry.principal && entry.principal !== account.principal) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: principal differs from saved roster; inspect deployment");
      entry.principal = account.principal; save();
      const args = { principal: account.principal, role, ...options.args?.(account) };
      const encodedArgs = canonical(args);
      if (entry.args && entry.args !== encodedArgs) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: provisioning arguments changed; inspect roster instead of silently reassigning roles");
      entry.args = encodedArgs; save();
      if (!entry.done) {
      const response = await request(`/m/${options.provision.name}`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", authorization: `Bearer ${operator}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ id: entry.id, args }) });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok || result.state !== "sent") throw Error(`E_STAGING_PROVISION: ${options.provision.name} refused (${result.why?.code ?? result.denied?.code ?? "invalid reply"}); supplied arguments: ${Object.keys(args).sort().join(", ")}; default provision arguments are principal and role; use args(account) for custom names; partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
      entry.done = true; save();
      }
      sessions[role]!.push(account);
    }
  }
  return sessions;
}

export function seedAccounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: SeedAccountsOptions): Promise<StagingSessions> {

function canonical(value: unknown): string {
  // Compare the JSON wire values, including nested objects, independent of key order.
  const sorted = (v: unknown): unknown => Array.isArray(v) ? v.map(sorted) : v && typeof v === "object"
    ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v).sort(([a],[b]) => a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0).map(([k,x]) => [k,sorted(x)])) : v;
  return JSON.stringify(sorted(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value))));
}
interface RosterEntry { role: string; index: number; email: string; password: string; id: string; principal?: string; args?: string; done: boolean }
interface Roster { version: 1; plan: string; entries: RosterEntry[] }
/** Reuse a named deployment roster with fresh password sessions; provision only unfinished entries.
 * @throws E_STAGING_INPUT for invalid plans; E_PRODUCTION_ACCOUNTS for staging/dev;
 * E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER for changed/unsafe roster or failed saved login; E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER_BUSY for concurrent setup;
 * E_STAGING_TIMEOUT, E_STAGING_SIGNUP or E_STAGING_PROVISION for incomplete setup.
 */
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#16-wave-27-process-checks-dx10 — persist credentials and stable IDs before production effects.
export async function provisionAccounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken"> & { deploymentDir?: string }, options: ProvisionAccountsOptions): Promise<ProvisionedSessions> {
  validatePlan(options);
  const name = options.roster ?? "default", directory = options.rosterDir ?? host.deploymentDir;
  if (!directory || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(name)) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: use startServe or supply rosterDir, and a simple roster name");
  const base = resolve(directory);
  if (!lstatSync(base).isDirectory()) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: rosterDir must be an existing deployment directory");
  const folder = join(base, "proof-accounts"); mkdirSync(folder, {mode:0o700, recursive:true});
  // Persist the newly created roster directory's link as well as each snapshot.
  const parent = openSync(base, "r"); try { fsyncSync(parent); } finally { closeSync(parent); }
  const path = join(folder, name + ".json"), lock = path + ".lock";
  let fd: number;
  try { fd = openSync(lock, "wx", 0o600); }
  catch { throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER_BUSY: roster already in use; after an interrupted driver inspect and remove its .lock before retrying"); }
  try {
    const plan = JSON.stringify({roles:Object.entries(options.roles).sort(), provision:options.provision.name, service:options.service ?? "operator"});
    let roster: Roster;
    try {
      if (!lstatSync(path).isFile() || (lstatSync(path).mode & 0o077)) throw Error("unsafe roster file");
      roster = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8"));
      if (roster.version !== 1 || roster.plan !== plan || !Array.isArray(roster.entries)) throw Error("roster plan changed");
      const slots = new Set<string>();
      for (const entry of roster.entries) {
        if (!entry) throw Error("invalid roster entry");
        const slot = `${entry.role}:${entry.index}`;
        if (!entry || typeof entry.role !== "string" || !Number.isSafeInteger(entry.index) || entry.index < 0
            || entry.index >= (options.roles[entry.role] ?? 0) || slots.has(slot)
            || typeof entry.email !== "string" || !entry.email.endsWith("@example.test") || typeof entry.password !== "string" || entry.password.length < 20
            || typeof entry.id !== "string" || !/^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/.test(entry.id) || typeof entry.done !== "boolean"
            || (entry.principal !== undefined && typeof entry.principal !== "string") || (entry.args !== undefined && typeof entry.args !== "string")
            || (entry.done && (!entry.principal || !entry.args))) throw Error("invalid roster entry");
        slots.add(slot);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "ENOENT") throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: unreadable, unsafe or changed roster; inspect it; no new accounts created");
      roster = {version:1, plan, entries:[]};
    }
    const save = () => {
      const temporary = path + "." + randomUUID() + ".tmp";
      const snapshot = openSync(temporary, "wx", 0o600);
      try { writeFileSync(snapshot, JSON.stringify(roster)); fsyncSync(snapshot); } finally { closeSync(snapshot); }
      renameSync(temporary, path);
      const parent = openSync(folder, "r"); try { fsyncSync(parent); } finally { closeSync(parent); }
    };
    return await accounts(host, options, "production", roster, save);
  } finally { closeSync(fd); unlinkSync(lock); }
}
